```text
# 008-家长端平板/横屏适配设计

| 项目 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| 编号 | 008 |
| 版本 | v1 |
| 状态 | done |
| 作者 | AI |
| 创建日期 | 2026-11-04 |
| 对应计划 | [008-家长端平板适配实施计划](../plans/008-家长端平板适配实施计划.md) |
| 前置 | 家长端 v0.4.0（Flutter） |

## 1. 背景与目标

- 家长端目前按手机竖屏设计，平板/横屏下留白大、列表单列；
- 第 4 期先从「多端一致性」中成本最低的平板/横屏适配开始；
- 非目标：小程序、孩子端独立 App、会员/支付（后续单独设计）。

## 2. 断点

| 宽度 | 形态 | 导航 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| < 720 | 手机 | 底部 NavigationBar |
| 720–1099 | 大屏手机/小平板 | 底部导航 + 内容限宽 |
| ≥ 1100 | 平板/横屏 | 左侧 NavigationRail + 内容最大 1200 |

## 3. 页面调整

- 首页：宽屏用 NavigationRail；内容居中限宽；
- 孩子/设备：宽屏两列卡片网格；
- 报告：宽屏左右分栏（最新报告 + 历史）；
- 规则/PIN/成员/登录：表单限宽 720–900；
- 诊断/告警：列表限宽 1000。

## 4. 验收

- 手机尺寸布局不回归；
- 横屏 1920×1200 下出现侧边导航、列表/卡片多列；
- Widget 测试覆盖宽屏断点。

## 5. 风险

| 风险 | 对策 |
| --- | --- |
| 宽屏内容拉得过长 | 统一 maxWidth |
| 小屏回归 | Widget 测试保留手机尺寸用例 |
| 刘海/安全区 | SafeArea 保留 |

## 6. 变更记录

| 日期 | 变更 | 原因 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-11-04 | 初稿并批准 | 第 4 期第一步 |

  unless File.exist?(generated_xcode_build_settings_path)
    raise "#{generated_xcode_build_settings_path} must exist. If you're running pod install manually, make sure flutter pub get is executed first"

  File.foreach(generated_xcode_build_settings_path) do |line|
    matches = line.match(/FLUTTER_ROOT\=(.*)/)
    return matches[1].strip if matches
  end
  raise "FLUTTER_ROOT not found in #{generated_xcode_build_settings_path}. Try deleting Generated.xcconfig, then run flutter pub get"
end

# OpenQuizGo 文档中心

本项目遵循两条铁律，详见根目录 [`AGENTS.md`](../AGENTS.md)：

1. 所有对话都留档；
2. 所有开发都先出设计方案与实施计划，并按计划推进。

## 目录导航

| 目录 | 用途 | 入口 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `docs/ROADMAP.md` | **总路线图与进度勾选（唯一勾选入口）** | [ROADMAP.md](ROADMAP.md) |
| `docs/idea.txt` | 产品需求草案（第 4–8 节，待补 1–3 节） | [idea.txt](idea.txt) |
| `api/openapi.yaml` | OpenAPI 3.0.3 接口契约 | [openapi.yaml](../api/openapi.yaml) |
| `docs/data-model.md` | 数据模型与迁移说明 | [data-model.md](data-model.md) |
| `docs/deployment.md` | 部署与运维手册 | [deployment.md](deployment.md) |
| `docs/security-checklist.md` | 上线安全自查 | [security-checklist.md](security-checklist.md) |
| `docs/acceptance/phase1.md` | 第一期验收报告 | [phase1.md](acceptance/phase1.md) |
| `docs/conversations/` | 对话留档（Markdown + 原始 JSON） | [INDEX.md](conversations/INDEX.md) |
| `docs/design/` | 设计方案 | [TEMPLATE.md](design/TEMPLATE.md) |
| `docs/plans/` | 实施计划 | [TEMPLATE.md](plans/TEMPLATE.md)、[BACKLOG.md](plans/BACKLOG.md) |
| `docs/auto-continue.md` | 10 分钟无人值守自动推进 | [auto-continue.md](auto-continue.md) |

## 常用命令

```bash
# 归档对话（会话结束前执行；会话开始时执行 --all 补档）
node scripts/archive-conversation.mjs
node scripts/archive-conversation.mjs --all
```

The archived conversation 002 titled "iOS 适配 iPad 自适应布局" is the current session's archive (created when I ran --all, using the current session title maybe from the user message). Interesting — 3 messages.

Now, design decisions. Before coding, need design + plan per rules. The user request is to implement iOS/iPad adaptation. Design 008 already covers tablet adaptation generically, but new design should be 076 (max is 075).

Let me check what exactly is insufficient:
- Breakpoints: iPad portrait 768-834 → not expanded, no rail. Need medium breakpoint handling: maybe 2-column layout at >=720 as design says, but currently child/device use >=900 for 2 columns and 1400 for 3. Report uses >=1000. For iPad portrait 768 → single column. Could improve.
- Navigation: use NavigationRail at >= 900 (iPad portrait) or use a hybrid: at medium use rail with labels? Design 008 says bottom nav 720–1099. Hmm, if the design says that, changing it requires updating design.

But also "开发苹果的ios端" implies iOS platform itself: maybe the iOS build doesn't work? Let me try `flutter build ios --no-codesign --simulator` or run analyze/test. Check plugins requiring iOS config: flutter_secure_storage needs Keychain Sharing? Usually works. shared_preferences fine.

Check the iOS deployment target: Flutter 3.44 requires iOS 13+ maybe 14? Fine.

Check app display name "Openquizgo Parent" should be "OpenQuizGo 家长端"? Info.plist CFBundleDisplayName. Also iPad app icon: missing 76x76, 83.5x83.5, 20x20 ipad? Let's check Contents.json. App Store requires iPad icons if device family includes iPad. Check which icons exist.

Also launch screen: default Flutter LaunchImage (blank white). Could add brand assets? docs/brand has logo. Might be part of iOS polish.

Let's check the app icons contents and brand folder.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import 'core/providers.dart';
import 'core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'features/lock/app_lock_gate.dart';

class OpenQuizGoApp extends ConsumerWidget {
  const OpenQuizGoApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final router = ref.watch(routerProvider);
    return MaterialApp.router(
      title: 'OpenQuizGo 家长端',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: AppTheme.light(),
      routerConfig: router,
      builder: (context, child) =>
          AppLockGate(child: child ?? const SizedBox.shrink()),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppTheme {
  static const seed = Color(0xFF1B8A6B);

  static ThemeData light() {
    final scheme = ColorScheme.fromSeed(
      seedColor: seed,
      brightness: Brightness.light,
    );
    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      colorScheme: scheme,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF6F8F7),
      appBarTheme: AppBarTheme(
        centerTitle: false,
        backgroundColor: scheme.surface,
        foregroundColor: scheme.onSurface,
        elevation: 0,
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        elevation: 0,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        color: Colors.white,
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: Colors.white,
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: scheme.outlineVariant),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: scheme.outlineVariant),
        ),
      ),
      filledButtonTheme: FilledButtonThemeData(
        style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
          minimumSize: const Size(0, 48),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

The iOS project looks reasonably complete. But there's no explicit iPad adaptive refinement beyond 008's baseline. The user says "开发苹果的ios端自适应ipad端" — likely they want iOS client developed and iPad adaptivity. Given no iOS platform-specific work in progress, I'd propose:

Design 076: iOS/iPad 适配
- Goals: iPad (all sizes) gets three-pane/rail layout, multi-column content, keyboard/safe area handling; iPhone unchanged; iOS polish (Cupertino back swipe, status bar, launch screen/branding, display name, orientation, app icon check).
- Breakpoints: phone <600, medium 600–899 (iPad portrait 744/768/820/834, iPhone landscape 844/852/932), expanded ≥900 (iPad landscape 1024+, iPad Pro 11 portrait 834? Actually iPad Pro 11" portrait = 834). Hmm careful: iPad portrait widths: mini 744, 10.2" 810, 10.9" 820, Pro 11 834, Pro 12.9 1024. iPhone landscape: 667–956. So width alone can't distinguish iPad from iPhone landscape. Need `MediaQuery.sizeOf` plus shortestSide >= 600 to identify tablet. That's the classic distinction.

So implement an `AdaptiveScaffold`/`DeviceType` helper:
- Use `MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).shortestSide >= 600` → tablet.
- Tablet: always NavigationRail (even portrait) because bottom nav on iPad is wasteful; content max width 1200; two columns in portrait ≥720 available content.
- iPhone: bottom nav; landscape iPhone maybe rail if height is short? Better keep bottom nav on iPhone to preserve behavior.
- Add "two-pane" master-detail on report at >=900.

Navigation rail on iPad portrait leaves less content width (768-80=688) → single column child cards still okay. Two-column at content width >= 700? Cards mainAxisExtent 168; 2 columns at 688 might be cramped but fine. Currently threshold is 900 for 2 cols; with rail it'd be stuck at 1 col. Adjust thresholds to 640 for 2 cols, 1100 for 3. Similarly device cards at 2 cols with 4.2 aspect → width ~330, okay.

Also settings/pin/members pages use ContentFrame.

Design doc content:
- Background: iOS app exists with default Flutter assets; iPad is included in device family but layout only switches at 1100 and iPad portrait gets phone layout; icons/launch screen are Flutter defaults.
- Goals: iPhone unchanged; iPad all sizes gets rail + multi-column; iPad multitasking (Split View/Slide Over) responds; brand assets; iOS config polish; tests.
- Non-goals: Apple signing/TestFlight (external cert), push notifications, IAP, native widgets, multi-window.
- Acceptance:
  - `flutter analyze` clean, `flutter test` pass, `flutter build ios --simulator` succeeds.
  - iPad Pro 12.9 (1024x1366 portrait) → rail, 2-col child/device; landscape 1366x1024 → rail extended? width 1366 - rail 256 → 1110 → 3 cols? With extended rail maybe. Let's define: extended rail when width >= 1200 (only large iPad landscape). Content width 1366-256=1110 → 3 columns (>=1100). Hmm 3 columns at 1110 content is fine (each ~360).
  - iPad 10.9 portrait 820x1180 → rail compact (80) + content 740 → 2 cols.
  - iPhone 15 (390x844) → bottom nav 1 col.
  - iPhone landscape (844x390) → bottom nav.
  - Slide Over 320x1024 → phone nav.
- Implementation:
  - `AdaptiveShell` widget to encapsulate nav.
  - `NavigationRail` extended at >=1200.
  - `AppBar` actions unchanged.
  - dialogs: maybe constrain forms. Let me add `dialogTheme` insetPadding? Actually default dialog on iPad fits content width. Long text may be wide but okay. Could add custom `AdaptiveDialog` no.
  - Forms: `ContentFrame(maxWidth: formMax)` already in pages; login uses a card maybe maxWidth 420.
- iOS assets:
  - generate AppIcon from logo-1024.png (need flatten alpha? hasAlpha check: logo-1024 likely no alpha? We'll check).
  - generate LaunchImage 1x/2x/3x from logo-512 padded, storyboard background #F6F8F7 and image size; ensure aspect.
  - display name localized: InfoPlist.strings zh-Hans "OpenQuizGo 家长端"; CFBundleLocalizations.
  - iPad orientations already all four; keep.
  - `UIApplicationSupportsMultipleScenes` false (single window) — keep.
  - Add `UILaunchStoryboardName`, present.
  - Add iPad `UIRequiresFullScreen`? no, keep multitasking.
- Risk: generating icons must be square and no alpha, 1024 marketing; launch image may stretch.
- Version: 1.0.2.

String _formatCachedAt(DateTime? at) {
  if (at == null) return '';
  return '${at.month}-${at.day} '
      '${at.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:'
      '${at.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
}

class _ReportBody extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _ReportBody({required this.query, required this.childName});

  final ReportQuery query;
  final String childName;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final reports = ref.watch(reportsProvider(query));
    return reports.when(
      loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      error: (error, _) => Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text('加载报告失败：$error'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            OutlinedButton(
              onPressed: () => ref.invalidate(reportsProvider(query)),
              child: const Text('重试'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      data: (cachedReports) {
        final items = cachedReports.data;
        if (items.isEmpty) {
          return Center(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.insights,
                  size: 64,
                  color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text('$childName 还没有${query.period == 'week' ? '本周' : '本月'}报告'),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                const Text('完成一次答题后，这里会展示答题与观看统计'),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }
        final banner = cachedReports.stale
            ? Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                child: Text(
                  '离线数据 · 最后同步 ${_formatCachedAt(cachedReports.cachedAt)}',
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.orange, fontSize: 13),
                ),
              )
            : null;
        final latest = _LatestReportCard(
          report: items.first,
          previous: items.length > 1 ? items[1] : null,
        );
        final history = [
          for (final report in items.skip(1)) _HistoryTile(report: report),
        ];
        return LayoutBuilder(
          builder: (context, constraints) {
            final wide = constraints.maxWidth >= 1000 && items.length > 1;
            if (wide) {
              return Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    flex: 2,
                    child: ListView(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 24),
                      children: [?banner, latest],

                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                  Expanded(
                    flex: 1,
                    child: ListView(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(0, 4, 16, 24),
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          '历史报告',
                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        ...history,
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              );

`TARGETED_DEVICE_FAMILY = \"1,2\"`，iPad 已包含在安装目标内；\n  - 导航仅在逻辑宽度 ≥ 1100 时切换为 `NavigationRail`，iPad 竖屏（744–1024）仍使用底部导航，横向空间被浪费；\n  - 孩子/设备卡片要到 900 才两列、1400 才三列，iPad 竖屏在侧边栏存在时经常只有一列；\n  - 报告页左右分栏阈值 1000，iPad 竖屏无法分栏；\n  - iOS App 图标仍是 Flutter 默认图标，启动图是 1×1 透明占位，桌面名称是 `Openquizgo Parent`；\n  - 008 的断点只按「宽度」判定，无法区分 iPhone 横屏与 iPad 竖屏（两者宽度区间重叠）。\n- iPad 支持 Split View / Slide Over / Stage Manager，窗口宽度可窄至 320，需要随窗口尺寸实时重排。\n\n### 1.2 本次目标（可验证）\n\n1. iOS 工程具备可发布的品牌资产：App 图标、启动图、中文桌面名称。\n2. 用「最短边」识别平板，iPad 全尺寸使用侧边导航；iPhone（含横屏）保持底部导航。\n3. iPad 内容区自适应：竖屏两列、横屏三列、报告页宽屏左右分栏。\n4. iPad 分屏 /  Slide Over 窄窗口自动退回手机布局，不出现挤压或溢出。\n5. `flutter analyze`、`flutter test`、`flutter build ios --simulator` 全部通过。\n\n### 1.3 非目标（明确不做什么）\n\n- Apple 开发者签名、TestFlight、App Store 提交流程（需证书，仍属外部阻塞）；\n- APNs 推送、Apple IAP、Sign in with Apple；\n- 原生 SwiftUI 页面、iPad 多窗口（多 Scene）、Apple Pencil / 键盘快捷键专项优化；\n- 改动服务端接口或数据结构。\n\n## 2. 需求与验收标准\n\n| 编号 | 需求 | 验收标准 | 优先级 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| R1 | 设备形态识别 | 最短边 ≥ 600 判定为平板；iPhone 横屏（如 844×390）仍为手机 | P0 |\n| R2 | iPad 导航 | 平板全部尺寸显示 `NavigationRail`，宽度 ≥ 1200 时展开显示文字标签 | P0 |\n| R3 | iPhone 导航不回归 | 手机尺寸显示底部 `NavigationBar`，无 `NavigationRail` | P0 |\n| R4 | 栅格自适应 | 内容宽度 < 640 单列、640–1099 两列、≥ 1100 三列 | P0 |\n| R5 | 报告分栏 | 内容宽度 ≥ 900 且历史报告 ≥ 1 条时左右分栏 | P1 |\n| R6 | 分屏窄窗口 | 窗口宽 320 时使用底部导航与单列，无溢出异常 | P1 |\n| R7 | iOS 品牌资产 | 图标为项目 Logo（无 alpha）、启动图为 Logo、桌面名称为「OpenQuizGo 家长端」 | P0 |\n| R8 | iPad 方向 | iPad 支持四个方向；iPhone 保持竖屏 + 左右横屏 | P1 |\n| R9 | 质量门禁 | analyze 0 issue、全部单测通过、iOS 模拟器构建成功 | P0 |\n\n## 3. 方案对比与选型\n\n| 方案 | 优点 | 缺点 | 结论 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| A. 仅按宽度断点（现状） | 改动最小 | iPhone 横屏与 iPad 竖屏混淆；iPad 竖屏仍用底部导航 | 否决 |\n| B. 按最短边识别平板 + 宽度决定栅格 | 判定稳定，手机/平板语义清晰，兼容分屏 | 需要新增一个布局工具与测试 | **采用** |\n| C. 为 iPad 单独维护一套原生/页面 | 最贴近 HIG | 双份代码，维护成本高，与 Flutter 单代码库策略冲突 | 否决 |\n| D. 引入第三方 adaptive 框架 | 开箱即用 | 增加依赖与学习成本，本项目布局诉求简单 | 否决 |\n\n选型理由：方案 B 在现有 `core/layout/adaptive.dart` 上增量演进，纯 Dart、无新依赖，可被 Widget 测试完整覆盖，同时保留 iPhone 现有体验。\n\n## 4. 总体架构\n\n### 4.1 模块划分与职责\n\n| 模块 | 职责 |\n| --- | --- |\n| `core/layout/adaptive.dart` | `FormFactor` 判定、断点常量、`gridColumns`、`ContentFrame` |\n| `core/layout/adaptive_shell.dart` | 统一导航骨架：手机底部导航 / 平板侧边导航，供 `HomeShell` 使用 |\n| `features/shell/home_shell.dart` | 组装导航项、消息入口、`MembershipBanner` 与页面 `IndexedStack` |\n| `features/child`、`features/device` | 使用统一 `gridColumns` 决定卡片列数 |\n| `features/report` | 使用 `Breakpoints.twoPane` 决定左右分栏 |\n| `ios/Runner` | 图标、启动图、Info.plist 名称与方向声明 |\n\n### 4.2 关键流程\n\n```text\nMediaQuery.size\n   │\n   ├─ shortestSide ≥ 600 ? ── 否 ──> 手机：底部 NavigationBar + 单列\n   │        是\n   └─> 平板：NavigationRail\n             ├─ width ≥ 1200 → 展开（图标 + 文字）\n             └─ width < 1200 → 紧凑（仅图标，仍显示标签）\n       内容区宽度 W\n             ├─ W < 640    → 1 列\n             ├─ 640–1099   → 2 列\n             └─ ≥ 1100     → 3 列\n```\n\n窗口尺寸变化（旋转、分屏、Stage Manager 缩放）由 `MediaQuery`/`LayoutBuilder` 驱动，无需监听原生事件。\n\n### 4.3 断点定义\n\n| 常量 | 值 | 用途 |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `tabletShortestSide` | 600 | 平板识别（最短边） |\n| `twoColumn` | 640 | 卡片两列 |\n| `twoPane` | 900 | 报告左右分栏 |\n| `threeColumn` | 1100 | 卡片三列 |\n| `extendedRail` | 1200 | 侧边栏展开 |\n| `contentMax` | 1200 | 内容最大宽度 |\n| `formMax` | 720 | 表单最大宽度 |\n| `listMax` | 1000 | 长列表最大宽度 |\n\n> 008 中「720–1099 使用底部导航」的规则由本设计替代；008 的手机/表单/限宽结论继续有效。\n\n### 4.4 目录结构影响\n\n```text\nparent/lib/core/layout/\n├── adaptive.dart          # 扩展：FormFactor / gridColumns / 新断点\n└── adaptive_shell.dart    # 新增：统一导航骨架\nparent/test/adaptive_test.dart      # 新增\nparent/test/home_shell_test.dart    # 由 smoke_test 中的宽屏用例迁出并扩充\n```\n\n无服务端、数据库、接
…（已截断 1263 字符，完整内容见原始 JSON）

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'adaptive.dart';

class AdaptiveNavItem {
  const AdaptiveNavItem({required this.icon, required this.label});
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
}

class AdaptiveShell extends StatelessWidget {
  const AdaptiveShell({
    super.key,
    required this.appBar,
    required this.body,
    required this.items,
    required this.selectedIndex,
    required this.onSelected,
  });

  final PreferredSizeWidget appBar;
  final Widget body;
  final List<AdaptiveNavItem> items;
  final int selectedIndex;
  final ValueChanged<int> onSelected;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final size = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context);
    final tablet = isTabletSize(size);
    if (!tablet) {
      return Scaffold(
        appBar: appBar,
        body: body,
        bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
          selectedIndex: selectedIndex,
          onDestinationSelected: onSelected,
          destinations: [for (final item in items) NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(item.icon), label: item.label)],
        ),
      );
    }
    final extended = size.width >= Breakpoints.extendedRail;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: appBar,
      body: Row(
        children: [
          NavigationRail(
            extended: extended,
            selectedIndex: selectedIndex,
            labelType: extended ? NavigationRailLabelType.none : NavigationRailLabelType.all,
            onDestinationSelected: onSelected,
            destinations: [for(...) NavigationRailDestination(icon: Icon(item.icon), label: Text(item.label))],
          ),
          const VerticalDivider(width: 1),
          Expanded(child: body),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Note when `extended: true`, labelType must be none (assert extended implies labelType == none). Set conditional.

Potential issue: On tablet, body ContentFrame center max 1200 within content area; rail outside. Fine.

But content max 1200. Let me set threeColumn = 1080, doc update. Actually grid at 1080 with 3 cols → 349 each; fine.

So 1080 gives mini 2 columns. Good.

  "content": "import 'package:flutter/material.dart';\n\n/// 设备形态：手机与平板使用不同的导航与栅格策略。\nenum FormFactor { phone, tablet }\n\nclass Breakpoints {\n  /// 最短边达到该值按平板处理，避免 iPhone 横屏被误判为平板。\n  static const double tabletShortestSide = 600;\n\n  /// 内容区宽度达到该值使用两列卡片。\n  static const double twoColumn = 640;\n\n  /// 内容区宽度达到该值报告页左右分栏。\n  static const double twoPane = 900;\n\n  /// 内容区宽度达到该值使用三列卡片。\n  static const double threeColumn = 1080;\n\n  /// 平板宽度达到该值展开侧边导航（显示文字）。\n  static const double extendedRail = 1200;\n\n  static const double contentMax = 1200;\n  static const double formMax = 720;\n  static const double listMax = 1000;\n}\n\nFormFactor formFactorOf(Size size) =>\n    size.shortestSide >= Breakpoints.tabletShortestSide\n    ? FormFactor.tablet\n    : FormFactor.phone;\n\nbool isTabletSize(Size size) => formFactorOf(size) == FormFactor.tablet;\n\nbool isTablet(BuildContext context) => isTabletSize(MediaQuery.sizeOf(context));\n\n/// 根据内容区可用宽度返回卡片列数。\nint gridColumns(double width) {\n  if (width >= Breakpoints.threeColumn) return 3;\n  if (width >= Breakpoints.twoColumn) return 2;\n  return 1;\n}\n\n/// 居中并限制最大宽度，避免平板/横屏内容被拉伸。\nclass ContentFrame extends StatelessWidget {\n  const ContentFrame({\n    super.key,\n    required this.child,\n    this.maxWidth = Breakpoints.contentMax,\n    this.padding = EdgeInsets.zero,\n  });\n\n  final Widget child;\n  final double maxWidth;\n  final EdgeInsets padding;\n\n  @override\n  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n    return Center(\n      child: ConstrainedBox(\n        constraints: BoxConstraints(maxWidth: maxWidth),\n        child: Padding(padding: padding, child: child),\n      ),\n    );\n  }\n}\n",

NSBitmapImageRep "Inconsistent set of values" with bytesPerRow 0 bitsPerPixel 0 and samplesPerPixel 4 hasAlpha false. Use bitsPerPixel: 32 explicitly? For hasAlpha false with 4 samples, inconsistent. Use samplesPerPixel 3, bitsPerPixel 24, bytesPerRow size*3. Or use CGContext directly with premultipliedLast and later write PNG, but alpha would exist (fully opaque but hasAlpha yes?). PNG with alpha channel where all pixels opaque: sips reports hasAlpha yes if channel exists. App Store requires no alpha channel. CGImageDestination with noneSkipLast and RGB colorspace → no alpha.

Use CGContext:
```swift
let cs = CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceRGB()
let ctx = CGContext(data: nil, width: size, height: size, bitsPerComponent: 8, bytesPerRow: 0, space: cs, bitmapInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo.noneSkipLast.rawValue)!
ctx.setFillColor(background.cgColor); ctx.fill(...)
// draw logo CGImage
let cg = logo.cgImage(forProposedRect: nil, context: nil, hints: nil)!
ctx.draw(cg, in: CGRect(...))
let image = ctx.makeImage()!
let dest = CGImageDestinationCreateWithURL(url as CFURL, UTType.png.identifier as CFString, 1, nil)!
CGImageDestinationAddImage(dest, image, nil)
CGImageDestinationFinalize(dest)
```
Need import ImageIO and UniformTypeIdentifiers.

For launch with alpha: CGContext with premultipliedLast, clear fill, draw. Or just reuse NSImage resizing? Use CGContext.

Also background sampling: NSBitmapImageRep from logo may read premultiplied; colorAt works.

Let me rewrite using CoreGraphics. Using Coordinate flip: CGContext origin bottom-left; draw image directly (not flipped) will draw logo upside down? CGContext draw CGImage draws upright? In CoreGraphics, drawing an image into a context places it with origin at bottom-left but image is not flipped (CGImage drawing is upright in default coordinate system for bitmap contexts? Actually CGImage draw in a bitmap context results in the image appearing upside down relative to UIImage because bitmap contexts are bottom-left origin). Since our logo is symmetric-ish? No, person figures would flip vertically. Need to flip:
```swift
ctx.translateBy(x: 0, y: CGFloat(size))
ctx.scaleBy(x: 1, y: -1)
ctx.draw(cg, in: CGRect(x:0,y:0,width:size,height:size))
```
That renders upright in CG bottom-left context. Standard.

stderr)\n  exit(1)\n}\n\nfunc writePNG(\n  size: Int, to url: URL, opaque: Bool\n) throws {\n  let colorSpace = CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceRGB()\n  let alphaInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo = opaque ? .noneSkipLast : .premultipliedLast\n  guard\n    let context = CGContext(\n      data: nil,\n      width: size,\n      height: size,\n      bitsPerComponent: 8,\n      bytesPerRow: 0,\n      space: colorSpace,\n      bitmapInfo: alphaInfo.rawValue\n    )\n  else {\n    throw NSError(domain: \"assets\", code: 1)\n  }\n  context.interpolationQuality = .high\n  context.translateBy(x: 0, y: CGFloat(size))\n  context.scaleBy(x: 1, y: -1)\n  let rect = CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: size, height: size)\n  if opaque {\n    context.setFillColor(background.cgColor)\n    context.fill(rect)\n  } else {\n    context.clear(rect)\n  }\n  context.draw(logoCG, in: rect)\n  guard let image = context.makeImage(),\n    let destination = CGImageDestinationCreateWithURL(\n      url as CFURL, UTType.png.identifier as CFString, 1, nil)\n  else {\n    throw NSError(domain: \"assets\", code: 2)\n  }\n  CGImageDestinationAddImage(destination, image, nil)\n  guard CGImageDestinationFinalize(destination) else {\n    throw NSError(domain: \"assets\", code: 3)\n  }\n}\n\nlet icons: [(String, Int)] = [\n  (\"Icon-App-20x20@1x.png\", 20),\n  (\"Icon-App-20x20@2x.png\", 40),\n  (\"Icon-App-20x20@3x.png\", 60),\n  (\"Icon-App-29x29@1x.png\", 29),\n  (\"Icon-App-29x29@2x.png\", 58),\n  (\"Icon-App-29x29@3x.png\", 87),\n (\"Icon-App-76x76@1x.png\", 76),\n  (\"Icon-App-76x76@2x.png\", 152),\n  (\"Icon-App-83.5x83.5@2x.png\", 167),\n  (\"Icon-App-1024x1024@1x.png\", 1024),\n]\n\nfor (name, size) in icons {\n  try (\"LaunchImage@3x.png\", 504),\n]\nfor (name, size) in launchImages {\n  try writePNG(\n    size: size, to: launchDir.appendingPathComponent(name), opaque: false)\n}\n\nprint(\n  \"已生成 \\(icons.count) 1.0;\n  addTearDown(tester.view.resetPhysicalSize);\n  addTearDown(tester.view.resetDevicePixelRatio);\n\n  await tester.pumpWidget(\n    ProviderScope(\n      overrides: [\n        alertsProvider.overrideWith(\n          (ref) async => AlertList(items: const [], unread: 0),\n        ),\n        childrenProvider.overrideWith(\n          (ref) async =>\n tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));\n}\n\nint _childColumns(WidgetTester tester) {\n  final grid = tester.widget<GridView>(\n    find.descendant(of: find.byType(ChildPage), matching: find.byType(GridView)),\n  );\n  return (grid.gridDelegate as SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount)\n      .crossAxisCount;\n}\n\nvoid main() {\n  setUp(() {\n findsOneWidget);\n    expect(find.byType(NavigationRail), findsNothing);\n    expect(_childColumns(tester), 1);\n  });\n\n  testWidgets('iPhone 横屏仍使用底部导航', (tester) async {\n    await     tester.widget<NavigationRail>(find.byType(NavigationRail)).extended,\n      isFalse,\n    );\n    expect(_childColumns(tester), 2);\n  });\n\n  testWidgets('iPad Pro 横屏展开侧栏并显示三列', (tester) async {\n    await _pumpHome(tester, const Size(1366, 1024));\n\n    expect(find.byType(NavigationRail), findsOneWidget);\n    expect(\n To be more compliant, could commit code first (1.0.2), then archive + commit docs (1.0.3), tag v1.0.3 and update docs references to 1.0.3. That's noisy and version docs inconsistent.

Alternative: final response then another archive? Can't after response ends. Common approach: archive right before final message; accept final message missing. The rule says re-running archive overwrites the same session; if next session runs --all, it will pick up the final message! Indeed session start rule runs --all to ensure the previous session's closing messages are archived. So archive now is fine; next session's --all adds remaining. 

But docs/plan state v1.0.2 which commit hook will produce; ensure hook bumps from 1.0.1 → 1.0.2. Verify VERSION currently 1.0.1. Yes.

Now, before commit, check git status for unrelated modified files (conversations from earlier --all, brand docs untracked from prior session? apps icons). We should not commit unrelated user work? The brand folder was untracked from previous session (docs/brand), and conversation archives. The previous session apparently didn't commit docs/brand? Git log shows "docs: 补充..." but status shows docs/brand untracked. Hmm, previous session may have left uncommitted. We shouldn't sweep unrelated changes into our commit blindly. Let's inspect status fully and diffs.

Also note there are modified conversation archives from --all (expected). The docs/brand is needed for icon generation (already used). Including docs/brand in our commit is reasonable since iOS assets derive from it, but it's user work from prior session. It's part of repo intended. I'd include it, but mention.

Let me check git status and diffs summary.

````text

## 技术栈

- 服务端：Go 1.25（模块化单体）、PostgreSQL、Redis
- 迁移：goose（SQL 内嵌，单二进制）
- 电视端：Android TV（Kotlin + Compose for TV）
- 家长端：Flutter

## 目录结构

```
cmd/api           HTTP 服务入口
cmd/migrate       数据库迁移入口
cmd/admin         管理端账号 CLI
cmd/worker        报告聚合定时任务
cmd/loadtest      本地压测工具
internal/config   环境变量配置
internal/httpapi  路由、中间件、统一响应
internal/platform 数据库 / Redis / 日志适配
migrations        SQL 迁移（goose）
android           T0 设备管控 PoC（Java，见 android/README.md）
tv                正式 Android TV 守护端（Kotlin/Compose TV，见 tv/README.md）
docs              设计、计划、对话归档
```

## 本地启动

前置：Go 1.25+、PostgreSQL 14+、Redis 6+。

> 国内网络下载依赖超时：`go env -w GOPROXY=https://goproxy.cn,direct`

```bash
cp .env.example .env
# 按本机情况修改 DATABASE_URL / REDIS_URL

createdb openquizgo
make migrate-up
make run
```

验证：

````

</details>

## 常用命令

```bash
make help           # 查看全部命令
make test           # 单元测试
make vet            # 静态检查
make build          # 编译到 bin/
make migrate-up     # 执行迁移
make migrate-down   # 回滚一个迁移
make dev            # 迁移并启动
```

## 容器启动

```bash
docker compose up --build
```

## 配置

| 变量 | 默认值 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `APP_ENV` | `dev` | `dev` 输出文本日志，其他输出 JSON |
| `APP_VERSION` | `dev` | 版本号，透传到 `/v1/meta` |
| `HTTP_ADDR` | `:8080` | 监听地址 |
| `DATABASE_URL` | 无（必填） | PostgreSQL DSN |
| `REDIS_URL` | `redis://localhost:6379/0` | Redis DSN |
| `DB_MAX_CONNS` | `10` | 连接池上限 |
| `LOG_LEVEL` | `info` | debug / info / warn / error |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT` | `10s` | 优雅退出超时 |
| `REPORT_INTERVAL` | `1h` | 报告 worker 聚合间隔 |
| `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` | 空 | OTLP HTTP 追踪端点，留空关闭 |
| `JWT_SECRET` | dev 默认值 | 访问令牌签名密钥，生产必须替换且 ≥32 字符 |
| `ACCESS_TOKEN_TTL` | `15m` | 访问令牌有效期 |
| `REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL` | `720h` | 刷新令牌有效期 |
| `DATA_ENCRYPTION_KEY` | dev 默认值 | 手机号字段加密密钥（base64 32 字节） |
| `PHONE_HASH_KEY` | dev 默认值 | 手机号查询哈希密钥 |
| `OFFLINE_LEASE_KEY` | dev 默认值 | 离线租约 Ed25519 种子（base64 32 字节） |
| `SMS_MODE` | `mock` | `mock` 固定验证码；`disabled` 关闭 |
| `SMS_MOCK_CODE` | `123456` | mock 模式验证码 |

## 已实现接口（T3）

```text
#!/bin/sh
# 铁律 3：每次提交自动升级版本（1.0.X）。
#
# 安装：make hooks
# 临时跳过：OQG_SKIP_VERSION_BUMP=1 git commit ...
set -eu

root=$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)
cd "$root"

if [ "${OQG_SKIP_VERSION_BUMP:-0}" = "1" ]; then
  echo "[version] 已按 OQG_SKIP_VERSION_BUMP=1 跳过版本升级"
  exit 0
fi

node scripts/bump-version.mjs

git add VERSION tv/app/build.gradle.kts parent/pubspec.yaml
===
#!/usr/bin/env node
// 版本自增：1.0.X → 1.0.(X+1)
//
// 铁律 3：每次提交必须升级版本（1.0.X）。本脚本由 scripts/git-hooks/pre-commit
// 自动调用，也可手动执行：
//
//   node scripts/bump-version.mjs            # 写入新版本
//   node scripts/bump-version.mjs --dry-run  # 只打印，不落盘
//
// 同步位置：
//   VERSION                        → 1.0.X
//   tv/app/build.gradle.kts        → versionName = "1.0.X"，versionCode = 100 + X
//   parent/pubspec.yaml            → version: 1.0.X+<100 + X>

import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';

const dryRun = process.argv.includes('--dry-run');
const root = execFileSync('git', ['rev-parse', '--show-toplevel'], {
  encoding: 'utf8',
}).trim();

const read = (path) => readFileSync(`${root}/${path}`, 'utf8');
const write = (path, content) => {
  if (!dryRun) writeFileSync(`${root}/${path}`, content);
};

const current = read('VERSION').trim();
const matched = /^1\.0\.(\d+)$/.exec(current);
if (!matched) {
  console.error(`VERSION 格式非法：${current}（必须为 1.0.X）`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const patch = Number(matched[1]) + 1;
const version = `1.0.${patch}`;
const build = 100 + patch;

const tvPath = 'tv/app/build.gradle.kts';
let tv = read(tvPath);

```

## 主要改动

**自适应布局**
- 新增 `parent/lib/core/layout/adaptive_shell.dart`：手机用底部导航，平板统一用侧边 `NavigationRail`；宽度 ≥1200 时自动展开显示文字。
- 重写 `parent/lib/core/layout/adaptive.dart`：按**最短边 ≥600** 识别平板，避免 iPhone 横屏误判；新增 `gridColumns` 统一栅格规则。
- `home_shell.dart` 接入新导航骨架。
- 孩子/设备卡片：<640 单列、640–1079 两列、≥1080 三列。
- 报告页：内容宽度 ≥900 时左右分栏。
- iPad Split View / Slide Over / Stage Manager 窄窗口会自动退回手机布局。

**iOS 品牌与配置**
- 用 `docs/brand/logo-1024.png` 重新生成全部 15 张 App 图标（18 个槽位，已压平无 alpha）。
- 重新生成 168/336/504 启动图，启动页背景改为 `#F6F8F7`。
- 桌面名称改为「OpenQuizGo 家长端」。
- 显式声明 `UIRequiresFullScreen=false`，保留 iPad 分屏能力。
- 新增可复用生成脚本 `parent/tool/generate_ios_assets.swift`。

**验证**
- `dart analyze lib test`：0 issue。
- `flutter test`：59 个测试全部通过（新增手机、平板竖屏/横屏、Slide Over 用例）。
- `flutter build ios --simulator --debug`：构建成功。
- 已在 iPad 13 模拟器安装运行，图标与名称显示正常。
- 说明：`flutter analyze` 在本机中文路径下会触发 Flutter LSP 崩溃，已改用 `dart analyze`。

**文档与发布**
- 新增 `docs/design/076-iOS与iPad适配设计.md`、`docs/plans/076-iOS与iPad适配实施计划.md`。
- 更新 `ROADMAP.md`、`README.md`、`008` 设计修订记录，并归档本次对话。
- 版本 `1.0.2`，已打 tag `v1.0.2`，GitHub 与 Gitee 均已同步。

遗留：真机签名、TestFlight 与 App Store 上架仍需 Apple 开发者证书。

Critical finding: default API base URL is `http://127.0.0.1:8080/`. If they archive from Xcode without `--dart-define=API_BASE_URL=...`, the shipped app points to localhost and ATS also blocks cleartext (NSAllowsLocalNetworking only for local). Also `appVersion = '0.29.0'` is stale; used where? Check. Maybe displayed in settings. Not blocking but inconsistent with 1.0.2. Could be shown in UI. Search usage.

### 1. 生产 API 地址

当前默认地址是本地开发地址：

```dart
// parent/lib/core/config.dart
defaultValue: 'http://127.0.0.1:8080/'
```

**如果直接打包，App 会连不上服务器，审核必然失败。** 必须用生产域名重新打包：

```bash
cd /Users/mengfeng/我的文档/源码/OpenQuizGo/parent
flutter build ipa --release \
  --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=https://你的生产域名/
```

生成的归档在：

```text
parent/build/ios/archive/Runner.xcarchive
```

然后：

```bash
open build/ios/archive/Runner.xcarchive
```

这会打开 Xcode Organizer，点 **Distribute App → App Store Connect → Upload** 即可。

### 2. Xcode 签名

工程里已有分发证书和团队：

- Team：`meng feng (R2TCRBA6NZ)`
- 分发证书：`Apple Distribution: meng feng`
- Bundle ID：`com.openquizgo.openquizgoParent`

在 Xcode 中检查 **Runner → Signing & Capabilities**：

- Team 选择 `meng feng (R2TCRBA6NZ)`
- 勾选 **Automatically manage signing**
- Bundle Identifier 保持 `com.openquizgo.openquizgoParent`
- 确认没有红色报错

### 3. 版本号已刷新

我刚刷新了 Flutter 配置，Xcode 里现在应为：

- Version：`1.0.2`
- Build：`102`

如果 Xcode 仍显示旧版本，执行：

```bash
cd parent
flutter build ios --config-only --release
```

### 4. App Store Connect 创建 App 记录

登录 [App Store Connect](https://appstoreconnect.apple.com) → **我的 App → + → 新建 App**：

- 平台：iOS
- 名称：OpenQuizGo 家长端
- 主要语言：简体中文
- Bundle ID：选择/创建 `com.openquizgo.openquizgoParent`（必须与工程完全一致）
- SKU：可填 `openquizgo-parent-ios`

没有 App 记录时，Xcode 上传会报 “No suitable application records”。

## 上传步骤

1. `flutter build ipa --release --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=https://生产域名/`
2. 打开 `build/ios/archive/Runner.xcarchive`
3. Organizer → **Distribute App**
4. 选择 **App Store Connect** → **Upload**
5. 保持默认选项，等待上传完成
6. 到 App Store Connect → TestFlight 查看构建处理状态

## 两个需要注意的问题

**iPad 截图需要重拍。** `dist/OpenQuizGo-AppStore-assets.zip` 里的 `ipad-13` 截图还是旧版底部导航布局；本次 076 改成侧边导航后，截图与实际上架版本不一致。iPhone 截图不受影响。

**设置页版本号仍是旧的。** `parent/lib/core/config.dart` 里 `appVersion = '0.29.0'`，设置页会显示这个值。不影响上传，但建议改成 `1.0.2` 或改为从包信息读取，避免审核看到版本不一致。

另外审核需要能登录体验，建议在 App Store Connect 的「审核备注」里提供测试手机号和验证码方式（或测试账号），否则手机号+验证码登录会阻断审核。

App Store 的「App Category」不在 Xcode 里设置，而是在 **App Store Connect → App 信息 → 类别** 里选。Xcode 只负责打包上传。

建议这样选：

| 项目 | 选择 |
| --- | --- |
| 主要类别 | **工具（Utilities）** |
| 次要类别 | **教育（Education）** |

理由：

- App 的核心价值是「家长管控电视使用时间、设备状态、远程解锁」，本质是**家长控制工具**，归到「工具」最准确；
- 「答题换时长」是手段，教育属性作为次要类别即可，兼顾教育类搜索；
- **不要选「儿童（Kids）」类别**：那是面向儿童使用的 App，会触发更严格的儿童审核规则，而这个 App 是家长使用的；
- App Store 没有「家长控制 / Parenting」这个独立类别。

配套建议：

- 年龄分级：**4+**（无不良内容；若担心远程解锁等描述，4+ 仍适用）；
- 不要勾选「Made for Kids」；
- 如果后续产品更强调题库与学习，可以把「教育」提为主要类别、工具作为次要类别。

在 App Store Connect 里路径是：

**App 信息 → 可编辑 → 类别 → 主要类别 / 次要类别**

void main() {
  testWidgets('PIN 页校验 4–6 位数字', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      const ProviderScope(child: MaterialApp(home: PinPage())),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(FilledButton, '保存 PIN'));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(find.text('请输入 4–6 位数字'), findsWidgets);
  });

  testWidgets('成员页展示角色与脱敏手机号', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      ProviderScope(
        overrides: [
          membersProvider.overrideWith(
            (ref) async => [
              FamilyMember(
                accountId: 'a1',
                phone: '139****2222',
                role: 'owner',
                joinedAt: DateTime(2026, 11, 4),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
        child: const MaterialApp(home: MembersPage()),
      ),
    );
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
    expect(find.text('139****2222'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('家庭管理员'), findsOneWidget);

## 不能直接 Archive 的原因

`parent/lib/core/config.dart` 默认地址仍是：

```text
http://127.0.0.1:8080/
```

如果在 Xcode 里直接 Archive，打出来的包会连本机，审核员无法登录。**必须用下面的方式打包**：

```bash
cd /Users/mengfeng/我的文档/源码/OpenQuizGo/parent

flutter build ipa --release \
  --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=https://你的生产域名/

open build/ios/archive/Runner.xcarchive
```

然后在打开的 Organizer 里：

**Distribute App → App Store Connect → Upload**

这样打出来的包才会使用生产 API，版本是 1.0.4（104）。

## 上传前 App Store Connect 还要填

这些不在 Xcode 里，但不影响先把包传上去（传完再补也行）：

- 创建 App 记录：名称「电视时间管家」、Bundle ID 与工程一致
- 副标题：`答题换时长 · 家长远程管控`
- 类别：主要「工具」，次要「教育」
- 年龄分级、App 隐私问卷
- iPhone / iPad 截图（现有截图名称和 iPad 布局已过时）
- 审核备注：提供一个可登录的测试手机号；否则审核员会卡在验证码登录

**结论**：Xcode 配置已经可以打包上传，但请用上面的 `flutter build ipa --dart-define=...` 命令，不要直接在 Xcode 里 Archive。把生产域名发我，我可以直接帮你把 IPA 打出来并检查归档是否正常。

## 🤖 助手 · 17:44:12

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

User asks: "所以我需要去申请云服务器和域名吗？" — Do I need to apply for/buy a cloud server and domain?

Context: App needs a production API endpoint for App Store. They need to host the Go backend, PostgreSQL, Redis, S3. So yes, for a real App Store release with login functionality, they need a publicly accessible HTTPS endpoint. Options:
1. Cloud server + domain + HTTPS (recommended): e.g., Alibaba Cloud/Tencent Cloud/Huawei Cloud; domain; ICP备案 (mainland China server requires ICP filing); TLS cert; deploy via Docker Compose / existing deployment docs.
2. Serverless / PaaS (Railway, Render, Fly.io, Zeabur, Cloud Run) + managed Postgres/Redis + object storage — avoids server ops, still need domain (or platform subdomain). For China users, overseas hosting may be slow/blocked; App Store review from US can access.
3. If app is only for personal/family use and not public, could use TestFlight with a temporary tunnel (Cloudflare Tunnel/ngrok) but review/availability unreliable; not suitable for App Store.
4. Apple review needs backend reachable 24/7; localhost won't work.

Need specifics: The backend is Go + PostgreSQL + Redis + S3-compatible storage + SMS provider. For production, need:
- 1 cloud server (2C4G enough for small scale) or container platform
- domain (must be ICP-filed if server in mainland China)
- HTTPS certificate (free Let's Encrypt or cloud free cert)
- Object storage for images/audio (OSS/COS or MinIO on server)
- SMS provider for login codes (Aliyun/Tencent SMS) — currently SMS_MODE=mock dev; production needs real SMS; or keep mock? Mock means anyone can log in with 123456; bad for production. Need real SMS or alternative login for review. This is important.
- Redis and PostgreSQL can be self-hosted Docker or managed.

Also App Store Connect doesn't require a domain per se, but the app needs an API endpoint. Apple's ATS requires HTTPS (TLS 1.2+).

Cost estimate:
- Domain .com ~¥60-100/year; .cn requires实名 and ICP.
- Cloud server 2C4G ~¥100-300/month (promos cheaper); managed DB extra.
- SMS ~¥0.04/条.
- OSS storage cheap.
- SSL free.

Mainland ICP filing takes 1-3 weeks, requires company/individual and domain; if they want to launch fast, Hong Kong/Singapore server avoids ICP but can be slower in mainland and may face connectivity issues. For App Store review (US), fine. For Chinese users, HK is okay-ish.

Alternative: use existing server if they have one. Ask if they already have a server/domain. Maybe the project's deployment docs assume a server.

Need answer: "是的，如果要正式上架并让用户能登录使用，必须有公网可访问的 HTTPS 后端；云服务器和域名是最直接方案。" Then give minimal setup and options/costs, plus what's needed in code/build:
- Set API_BASE_URL to https://api.yourdomain.com/
- Deploy per docs/deployment.md (Docker Compose, migrations, env: DATABASE_URL, REDIS_URL, JWT_SECRET, DATA_ENCRYPTION_KEY, PHONE_HASH_KEY, OFFLINE_LEASE_KEY, SMS_MODE, APP_VERSION).
- Nginx TLS config.
- S3 bucket.
- Real SMS or demo account.

Also mention App Store review demo account: if no real SMS, create a test account with a fixed code? The backend SMS_MODE=mock allows code 123456 for any phone; for production that's insecure. Could add an allowlist demo phone or review code. That's a code change; mention.

Also possible to reduce scope: If they just want to test upload/TestFlight now, they can upload a build pointing to a temporary public endpoint; but for App Review, backend must be stable.

Also note: App Store doesn't require the domain to be registered under the same company; just HTTPS.

Should I ask if they have existing server/domain? Answer directly with branches. Use question tool? The user asks a yes/no; answer with explanation, no need to ask. But could end with "如果你已有服务器/域名，告诉我，我可以帮你列出部署清单并生成配置". Don't over-ask.

````text
# 部署与运维手册

> 适用版本：0.1.0（第一期服务端）
> 对应设计：[001-项目总体设计方案](design/001-项目总体设计方案.md)

## 1. 组件

| 组件 | 说明 | 必需 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `api` | HTTP 服务（家长端、电视端、管理端） | 是 |
| `worker` | 报告聚合定时任务 | 是（可与 api 合并部署） |
| PostgreSQL | 唯一事实来源，建议 16 | 是 |
| Redis | 验证码、限流、设备激活码 | 是 |
| Prometheus | 指标抓取与告警 | 推荐 |
| 反向代理 | TLS 终止、限流、访问控制 | 生产必需 |

## 2. 环境变量

| 变量 | 必填 | 默认 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `APP_ENV` | 否 | `dev` | `dev` 输出文本日志；其他输出 JSON |
| `APP_VERSION` | 否 | `dev` | 透传到 `/v1/meta` |
| `HTTP_ADDR` | 否 | `:8080` | 监听地址 |
| `DATABASE_URL` | **是** | — | PostgreSQL DSN |
| `REDIS_URL` | 否 | `redis://localhost:6379/0` | Redis DSN |
| `DB_MAX_CONNS` | 否 | `10` | 连接池上限 |
| `LOG_LEVEL` | 否 | `info` | debug/info/warn/error |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT` | 否 | `10s` | 优雅退出 |
| `JWT_SECRET` | 生产必填 | dev 默认 | 家长/管理端/授权令牌签名，≥32 字符 |
| `ACCESS_TOKEN_TTL` | 否 | `15m` | 家长访问令牌 |
| `REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL` | 否 | `720h` | 刷新令牌 |
| `DATA_ENCRYPTION_KEY` | 生产必填 | dev 默认 | 手机号 AES-256-GCM 密钥（base64 32 字节） |
| `PHONE_HASH_KEY` | 生产必填 | dev 默认 | 手机号 HMAC 密钥 |
| `OFFLINE_LEASE_KEY` | 生产必填 | dev 默认 | 离线租约 Ed25519 种子（base64 32 字节） |
| `SMS_MODE` | 否 | `mock` | 生产禁止 `mock`，需接入真实短信通道后设为 `disabled` 或扩展实现 |
| `PAYMENT_CALLBACK_SECRET` | 是 | dev 默认 | 支付回调 HMAC 密钥；生产必须显式配置，留空则禁用回调 |
| `REFUND_CALLBACK_SECRET` | 否 | 回退支付密钥 | 退款回调 HMAC 密钥；与支付密钥都为空则禁用退款回调 |
| `UPLOAD_DIR` | 否 | `./data/uploads` | 题目图片本地存储目录 |
| `UPLOAD_MAX_MB` | 否 | `5` | 单张题目图片大小上限（MB） |
| `SMS_MOCK_CODE` | 否 | `123456` | 仅 dev |
| `REPORT_INTERVAL` | 否 | `1h` | worker 聚合间隔 |
| `DEVICE_STALE_DAYS` | 否 | `30` | 设备多少天无活动后自动解绑；`0` 关闭 |
| `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` | 否 | 空 | OTLP/HTTP 追踪端点，留空关闭 |

> 生产启动会校验非 dev 环境的密钥与 `SMS_MODE`，缺失或仍为 mock 将拒绝启动。

## 3. 本地运行

```bash
cp .env.example .env
# 修改 DATABASE_URL / REDIS_URL
createdb openquizgo
make migrate-up
make run
```

初始化管理员：

```bash
go run ./cmd/admin create -username admin -password '********' -role super_admin -name 管理员
```

报告 worker：

```bash
make build
./bin/worker
```

## 4. Docker Compose

```bash
docker compose up --build
```

包含：PostgreSQL、Redis、API（启动前自动迁移）、worker、Prometheus（:9090）。

- API：http://localhost:8080
- Prometheus：http://localhost:9090
- 指标：http://localhost:8080/metrics

## 5. 迁移

```bash
make migrate-up       # 应用全部迁移
make migrate-status   # 查看版本
make migrate-down     # 回滚一个（仅开发/预发）
```

- 迁移内嵌在 `migrate` 二进制，生产无需额外文件。
- 发布顺序：备份 → 迁移 → 启动新版本 → 验证 `/readyz`。
- 回滚：镜像回退 + 按需执行 down；数据迁移默认只向前。

## 6. 反向代理（Nginx 示例）

```nginx
server {
    listen 443 ssl http2;
    server_name api.example.com;

    ssl_certificate     /etc/ssl/api.crt;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/ssl/api.key;

    location /metrics {
        allow 10.0.0.0/8;
        deny all;
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;
    }

    location / {
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_read_timeout 30s;
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;
    }
}
```

- 只暴露 API 与必要端口；PostgreSQL/Redis 不对外。
- `/metrics` 仅允许监控网段。
- 管理端建议独立域名/VPN。

## 7. 监控与告警

- 抓取配置：[`deploy/prometheus/prometheus.yml`](../deploy/prometheus/prometheus.yml)
- 告警规则：[`deploy/prometheus/alerts.yml`](../deploy/prometheus/alerts.yml)
- 关键指标：
  - `openquizgo_http_request_duration_seconds` p95

````

// SendLoginCode 下发登录验证码；mock 模式下写日志并返回固定验证码。
func (s *Service) SendLoginCode(ctx context.Context, phone string) error {
	normalized, err := normalizePhone(phone)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if s.opts.SMSMode == "disabled" {
		return ErrSMSDisabled
	}

	phoneHash := s.cipher.Hash(normalized)
	if err := s.checkSMSRate(ctx, phoneHash); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	code := s.opts.SMSMockCode
	if code == "" {
		code, err = randomDigits(6)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if err := s.redis.Set(ctx, smsKey(phoneHash), code, smsCodeTTL).Err(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("store sms code: %w", err)
	}

	if s.opts.Environment == "dev" {
		s.logger.Info("sms code issued (mock)", "phone", maskPhone(normalized), "code", code)
	}
	return nil
}

// Login 校验验证码并登录；手机号首次登录时自动创建账号与家庭。

## 最低配置清单

| 项目 | 说明 | 大概成本 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 云服务器 | 2 核 4G，Ubuntu/Debian + Docker，跑 API/worker/PostgreSQL/Redis | ¥100–300/月，活动机更便宜 |
| 域名 | 如 `api.你的域名.com`，解析到服务器 | ¥60–100/年 |
| HTTPS 证书 | Let's Encrypt 免费证书即可 | 免费 |
| 对象存储 | 题目图片/音频，可用云 OSS/COS，也可先本地目录 | 几元/月起 |
| 短信服务 | 登录验证码，阿里云/腾讯云短信 | 约 ¥0.04/条 |

部署本身项目里已有现成方案：`docker-compose.yml`、`docs/deployment.md`、Nginx 反代示例，顺利的话半天能跑起来。

## 但只有服务器还不够

这三件事是目前上架前真正的卡点：

**1. 短信登录还没有真实实现**

后端目前只支持：

```text
SMS_MODE=mock      # 生产环境直接拒绝启动
SMS_MODE=disabled  # 启动可以，但用户永远收不到验证码，无法登录
```

也就是说，**必须先接入阿里云/腾讯云短信**，否则服务器和域名都准备好了，审核员也登不进去。短信签名和模板审核通常还要 1–2 天。

**2. App 里还是「模拟支付」**

会员页面明确写着「模拟支付，不会产生真实扣款」。App Store 审核看到这个，轻则按「功能不完整」拒绝，重则按「数字内容必须走 IAP」拒绝。上架前要么在 iOS 版隐藏会员购买入口，要么接入 Apple IAP。

**3. 中国大陆上架需要备案**

- 服务器在大陆：域名要 ICP 备案；
- 上架中国区 App Store：还需要 **App 备案号**，App Store Connect 会要求填写；
- 想跳过备案可以选香港/新加坡服务器，但大陆用户访问速度和稳定性会差一些。

## 两种落地路线

**路线 A：正式上架（推荐）**

阿里云/腾讯云 2C4G + 域名 + 免费 SSL + 真实短信 + 备案。适合长期运营，周期约 2–3 周（主要等备案和短信审核）。

**路线 B：先跑通 TestFlight / 海外审核**

用 Railway、Render、Fly.io、Zeabur 这类 PaaS，自带 HTTPS 域名，配托管 PostgreSQL/Redis。不需要自己买服务器，也不需要域名备案；但还是要解决真实短信登录，否则审核员进不去。

## 建议的下一步

如果你已经有服务器和域名，把系统和配置告诉我，我可以直接给你一份生产 `.env`、Nginx/证书配置和部署命令。

如果还没有，建议先确认两件事再买：

1. 主要面向国内还是海外用户（决定是否需要备案）；
2. 短信服务准备用阿里云还是腾讯云（决定登录改造方案）。

确认后我可以把「服务器 + 域名 + 短信 + iOS 生产打包」整理成一份可执行的上架清单。

package com.openquizgo.guard.platform

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.IBinder
import android.os.Looper
import android.widget.Toast
import com.openquizgo.guard.GuardApplication
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.foreground.ForegroundState
import com.openquizgo.guard.domain.GuardState
import com.openquizgo.guard.ui.BlockerActivity
import com.openquizgo.guard.ui.MainActivity
import com.openquizgo.guard.ui.QuizActivity
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

class GuardService : Service() {

    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default)
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private var stateJob: Job? = null
    private var promptJob: Job? = null
    private var watchdogJob: Job? = null
    private var engine: com.openquizgo.guard.domain.GuardEngine? = null
    private var quizRequiredSince = 0L
    private var lastGuardLaunchAt = 0L

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        GuardLog.i("service", "guard service created")
        createChannel()
        startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, buildNotification("守护端启动中"))
        val container = (application as GuardApplication).container
        engine = container.guardEngine
        scope.launch {
            delay(1_500)
            container.diagnosticsReporter.reportCapabilityNow()
        }
        stateJob = scope.launch {
            engine?.state?.collect { state ->
                updateNotification(label(state))
                when (state) {
                    GuardState.QUIZ_REQUIRED -> {
                        if (quizRequiredSince == 0L) quizRequiredSince = System.currentTimeMillis()
                        startPromptLoop()
                    }
                    GuardState.BLOCKED -> {
                        quizRequiredSince = 0L
                        promptJob?.cancel()
                        GuardAccessibilityService.disarmQuiz()
                        launchBlocker()
                    }
                    GuardState.GRANTED -> {
                        quizRequiredSince = 0L
                        promptJob?.cancel()
                        GuardAccessibilityService.disarmQuiz()
                        toast("按确认键继续观看")
                    }
                    else -> {
                        quizRequiredSince = 0L
                        promptJob?.cancel()
                        GuardAccessibilityService.disarmQuiz()
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        watchdogJob = scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(WATCHDOG_INTERVAL_MS)
                watchdogTick()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun watchdogTick() {
        val current = engine?.state?.value ?: return
        val foreground = (application as GuardApplication).container.foregroundDetector.state.value
        when (current) {
            GuardState.BLOCKED -> {
                if (blockerEscaped(foreground) && !BlockerActivity.isShowing &&
                    nowSinceLaunch() > GUARD_RELAUNCH_GAP_MS
                ) {
                    GuardLog.w("watchdog: blocker missing pkg=${foreground.packageName}")
                    launchBlocker()
                }
            }
            GuardState.QUIZ_REQUIRED -> {
                if (!Permissions.accessibilityEnabled(this) ||
                    !GuardAccessibilityService.isConnected()
                ) {
                    return
                }
                GuardAccessibilityService.armQuiz()
                val waited = System.currentTimeMillis() - quizRequiredSince
                if (waited > QUIZ_FALLBACK_MS && guardTarget(foreground) &&
                    nowSinceLaunch() > GUARD_RELAUNCH_GAP_MS
                ) {
                    GuardLog.w("watchdog: quiz fallback after ${waited}ms")
                    launchQuiz()
                }
            }
            else -> Unit
        }
    }

    /** 阻挡态：除家长需要的系统设置外，任何前台都重新拉起阻挡页。 */
    private fun blockerEscaped(foreground: ForegroundState): Boolean {
        val pkg = foreground.packageName ?: return false
        if (pkg == packageName) return false
        return pkg !in ALLOWED_WHILE_BLOCKED
    }

    private fun guardTarget(foreground: ForegroundState): Boolean {
        val pkg = foreground.packageName ?: return false
        if (pkg == packageName) return false
        if (pkg in IGNORED_PACKAGES) return false
        return foreground.mediaActive || pkg in MEDIA_PACKAGES
    }

    private fun nowSinceLaunch(): Long = System.currentTimeMillis() - lastGuardLaunchAt

    private fun launchBlocker() {
        lastGuardLaunchAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val intent = Intent(this, BlockerActivity::class.java)
            .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP)
        runCatching { startActivity(intent) }
            .onFailure { GuardLog.e("service", "blocker launch failed", it) }
    }

    private fun launchQuiz() {
        lastGuardLaunchAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val intent = Inten

package com.openquizgo.guard.domain

import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.OfflineEntry
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.watch.PendingOffline
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.watch.StoredGrant
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.watch.StoredLease
import com.openquizgo.guard.platform.GuardLog
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.distinctUntilChanged
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import java.time.Instant

/** 答题通过后下发的授权信息。 */
data class GrantInfo(
    val grantId: String,
    val minutes: Int,
    val remainingSeconds: Int,
    val validUntilMillis: Long,
)

/**
 * 守护状态机（T4/T7）：
 * UNBOUND -> IDLE -> WATCHING -> QUIZ_REQUIRED -> QUIZZING -> GRANTED/BLOCKED
 *
 * T7 起以服务端授权为准：本地倒计时 + 30s 心跳校正；
 * 暂停（无媒体播放）不计入观看时长；断网期间按离线租约累计，联网后补报。
 */
class GuardEngine(
    private val preferences: GuardStorage,
    private val foregroundDetector: ForegroundSource,
    private val watchRepository: WatchGateway,
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
    private val clock: () -> Long = System::currentTimeMillis,
) {
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(GuardState.UNBOUND)
    val state: StateFlow<GuardState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private val _watchSeconds = MutableStateFlow(0L)
    val watchSeconds: StateFlow<Long> = _watchSeconds.asStateFlow()

    private val _grantMinutes = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val grantMinutes: StateFlow<Int> = _grantMinutes.asStateFlow()

    /** 当前授权剩余秒数（本地倒计时，心跳后以服务端为准）。 */
    private val _grantSeconds = MutableStateFlow(0L)
    val grantSeconds: StateFlow<Long> = _grantSeconds.asStateFlow()

    /** 进入 BLOCKED 的原因（quiz_failed / time_window / revoked）。 */
    private val _blockReason = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val blockReason: StateFlow<String?> = _blockReason.asStateFlow()

    private var grantId: String? = null
    private var grantValidUntilMillis: Long = 0
    private var lastHeartbeatMillis: Long = 0
    private var offline = false

    @Volatile
    private var lease: StoredLease? = null

    @Volatile
    private var pending: PendingOffline? = null

    @Volatile
    private var reportingPending = false

    @Volatile
    private var syncingGrant = false

    init {
        scope.launch { restore() }
        scope.launch {
            preferences.deviceToken.distinctUntilChanged().collect { token ->
                if (token.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    transition(GuardState.UNBOUND)
                } else {
                    if (_state.value == GuardState.UNBOUND) {
                        transition(GuardState.IDLE)
                    }
                    refreshLease()
                }
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            foregroundDetector.state.collect { foreground ->
                val current = _state.value
                if (_state.value == GuardState.UNBOUND) return@collect
                if (foreground.mediaActive && (current == GuardState.IDLE || current == GuardState.GRANTED)) {
                    transition(GuardState.WATCHING)
                    if (!hasUsableGrant()) {
                        transition(GuardState.QUIZ_REQUIRED)
                    }
                } else if (!foreground.mediaActive && current == GuardState.WATCHING) {
                    transition(GuardState.IDLE)
                }
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(1_000)
                tick()
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS)
                heartbeat()
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(UNLOCK_POLL_INTERVAL_MS)
                pollUnlockGrant()
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(10_000)
                persist()
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(LEASE_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS)
                refreshLease()
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(PENDING_RETRY_INTERVAL_MS)
                if (pending != null) reportPendingOffline()
            }
        }
    }

    fun onQuizStarted() {
        transition(GuardState.QUIZZING)
    }

    fun onQuizPassed(grant: GrantInfo?) {
        _watchSeconds.value = 0
        if (grant != null) {
            adoptGrantInternal(grant)
        } else {
            grantId = null
            _grantSeconds.value = 0
            _grantMinutes.value = 0
        }
        _blockReason.value = null
        transition(GuardState.GRANTED)
    }

    /** 家长端/后台签发新授权后由阻挡页调用，直接恢复观看。 */
    fun adoptGrant(grant: GrantInfo) {
        GuardLog.i("grant", "adopt id=${grant.grantId} minutes=${grant.minutes}")
        _watchSeconds.value = 0
        adoptGrantInternal(grant)
        _blockReason.value = null
        transition(GuardState.GRANTED)
        if (foregroundDetector.state.value.mediaActive) {
            transition(GuardState.WATCHING)
        }
    }

    private fun adoptGrantInternal(grant: GrantInfo) {
        grantId = grant.grantId
        grantValidUntilMillis = grant.validUntilMillis
        _grantSeconds.value = grant.remainingSeconds.toLong().coerceAtLeast(0)
        _grantMinutes.value = grant.minutes
        lastHeartbeatMillis = clock()
        offline = false
        scope.launch {
            preferences.saveStoredGrant(
                StoredGrant(
                    grantId = grant.grantId,
                    minutes = grant.minutes,
                    remainingSeconds = grant.remainingSeconds,
                    validUntilMillis = grant.validUntilMillis,
                ),
            )
        }

```text
package com.openquizgo.guard.data.quiz

import com.google.gson.Gson
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.local.GuardPreferences
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.AnswerResult
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.AnswerSubmission
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.ApiErrorBody
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.GuardApi
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.SessionQuestion
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.SessionSummary
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import retrofit2.Response

/** 服务端返回的业务错误（携带 code / request_id / session_id）。 */
class QuizApiException(
    val code: String,
    override val message: String,
    val sessionId: String? = null,
    val httpStatus: Int = 0,
) : Exception(message)

data class QuizSession(
    val sessionId: String,
    val expiresAt: String?,
    val passThreshold: Double,
    val questions: List<SessionQuestion>,
)

data class QuizOutcome(
    val summary: SessionSummary,
    val passed: Boolean,
) {
    val grantMinutes: Int get() = summary.grant?.minutes ?: 0
}

/**
 * 答题会话仓库（P2-T6）。
 *
 * 负责创建会话、断线恢复、提交答案与结算；所有请求使用设备令牌。
 */
class QuizRepository(
    private val api: GuardApi,
    private val preferences: GuardPreferences,
) {
    private val gson = Gson()

    /** 创建会话；若服务端提示已有会话，则尝试恢复该会话。 */
    suspend fun startSession(): QuizSession {
        val authorization = authorization()
        val response = api.createQuizSession(authorization)
        if (response.isSuccessful) {
            val body = response.body()
                ?: throw QuizApiException("EMPTY_RESPONSE", "服务端返回为空", httpStatus = response.code())
            return QuizSession(
                sessionId = body.sessionId,
                expiresAt = body.expiresAt,
                passThreshold = body.passThreshold,
                questions = body.questions.orEmpty(),
            )
        }
        val error = parseError(response)
        if (error.code == "QUIZ_SESSION_ACTIVE" && !error.sessionId.isNullOrBlank()) {
            return resumeSession(error.sessionId)
        }
        throw error
    }

    private suspend fun resumeSession(sessionId: String): QuizSession {
        val summary = requestOrThrow(api.quizSession(authorization(), sessionId))
        if (summary.state != "active") {
            throw QuizApiException(
                code = "QUIZ_SESSION_CLOSED",
                message = "答题会话已结束，请重新发起",
                sessionId = sessionId,
            )
        }
        return QuizSession(
            sessionId = summary.sessionId,
            expiresAt = summary.expiresAt,
            passThreshold = summary.passThreshold,
            questions = summary.questions.orEmpty(),
        )
    }

    suspend fun submitAnswer(
        sessionId: String,
        token: String,
        optionIds: List<String>,
    ): AnswerResult = requestOrThrow(
        api.submitQuizAnswer(
            authorization(),
            sessionId,
            AnswerSubmission(token = token, answer = optionIds),
        ),
    )

    suspend fun finish(sessionId: String): SessionSummary =
        requestOrThrow(api.finishQuizSession(authorization(), sessionId))

    private suspend fun authorization(): String {
        val token = preferences.deviceToken.first()
        if (token.isNullOrBlank()) {
            throw QuizApiException("AUTH_REQUIRED", "设备未激活，请先完成激活绑定", httpStatus = 401)
        }
        return "Bearer $token"
    }

    private fun <T> requestOrThrow(response: Response<T>): T {
        if (response.isSuccessful) {
            return response.body()
                ?: throw QuizApiException("EMPTY_RESPONSE", "服务端返回为空", httpStatus = response.code())
        }
        throw parseError(response)
    }

    private fun parseError(response: Response<*>): QuizApiException {
        val raw = runCatching { response.errorBody()?.string() }.getOrNull()
        val body = raw?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let {
            runCatching { gson.fromJson(it, ApiErrorBody::class.java) }.getOrNull()
        }
        val fallback = when (response.code()) {
            401 -> "设备未授权，请重新激活"
            404 -> "请求的资源不存在"
            409 -> "当前状态不允许该操作"
            410 -> "答题已超时，请重新发起"
            429 -> "操作过于频繁，请稍后再试"
            else -> "网络请求失败（HTTP ${response.code()}）"
        }
        return QuizApiException(
            code = body?.code ?: "HTTP_${response.code()}",
            message = body?.message?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: fallback,
            sessionId = body?.sessionId,
            httpStatus = response.code(),
        )
    }
}

package com.openquizgo.guard.ui

import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.foreground.ForegroundDetector
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.foreground.ForegroundState
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.local.GuardPreferences
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.ActivationCodeRequest
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.DeviceTokenRequest
import com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote.GuardApi
import com.openquizgo.guard.domain.GuardEngine

import com.openquizgo.guard.di.AppContainer
import com.openquizgo.guard.platform.DiagnosticsReporter
import com.openquizgo.guard.platform.GuardLog
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import retrofit2.HttpException

sealed interface GuardUiState {
    data object Loading : GuardUiState
    data class Activation(
        val code: String,
        val expiresInSeconds: Int,
        val deviceId: String,
        val expired: Boolean = false,
    ) : GuardUiState
    data class Bound(val childName: String) : GuardUiState
    data class Failure(val message: String) : GuardUiState
}

data class ServerStatus(
    val checking: Boolean = false,
    val ok: Boolean? = null,
    val message: String = "未检测",
)

class GuardViewModel(
    private val preferences: GuardPreferences,
    private val api: GuardApi,
    foregroundDetector: ForegroundDetector,
    guardEngine: GuardEngine,
    private val diagnostics: DiagnosticsReporter,
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow<GuardUiState>(GuardUiState.Loading)
    val state: StateFlow<GuardUiState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    val foregroundState: StateFlow<ForegroundState> = foregroundDetector.state
    val guardState: StateFlow<GuardState> = guardEngine.state
    val watchSeconds: StateFlow<Long> = guardEngine.watchSeconds
    val grantSeconds: StateFlow<Long> = guardEngine.grantSeconds
    val logs: StateFlow<List<String>> = GuardLog.recent

    private val _serverStatus = MutableStateFlow(ServerStatus())
    val serverStatus: StateFlow<ServerStatus> = _serverStatus.asStateFlow()

    private val _reportStatus = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val reportStatus: StateFlow<String?> = _reportStatus.asStateFlow()

    private var pollJob: Job? = null

    init {
        bootstrap()
    }

    fun regenerate() {
        startActivation()
    }

    fun pingServer() {
        if (_serverStatus.value.checking) return
        _serverStatus.value = ServerStatus(checking = true, message = "检测中…")
        viewModelScope.launch {
            runCatching { api.meta() }
                .onSuccess {
                    _serverStatus.value = ServerStatus(
                        ok = true,
                        message = "正常 · ${it.service} ${it.version}",
                    )
                }
                .onFailure {
                    _serverStatus.value = ServerStatus(
                        ok = false,
                        message = it.message ?: "连接失败",
                    )
                }
        }
    }

    fun reportCapabilityNow() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _reportStatus.value = "上报中…"
            val result = diagnostics.reportCapabilityNow()
            _reportStatus.value = if (result.isSuccess) "能力已上报" else "上报失败：${result.exceptionOrNull()?.message}"
        }
    }

    fun uploadDiagnostics() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _reportStatus.value = "上传日志中…"
            val result = diagnostics.uploadLogs()
            _reportStatus.value = if (result.isSuccess) {
                "诊断日志已上传"
            } else {
                "上传失败：${result.exceptionOrNull()?.message}"
            }
        }
    }

    fun resetBinding() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            pollJob?.cancel()
            GuardLog.i("binding", "binding reset by user")
            val token = preferences.deviceToken.first()
            if (!token.isNullOrBlank()) {
                runCatching { api.unbindDeviceMe("Bearer $token") }
                    .onFailure { GuardLog.w("binding", "server unbind failed: ${it.message}") }
            }
            preferences.clearBinding()
            startActivation()
        }
    }

    private fun bootstrap() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val token = preferences.deviceToken.first()
            if (token.isNullOrBlank()) {
                GuardLog.i("auth", "no device token, start activation")
                startActivation()
                return@launch
            }
            val cachedName = preferences.childName.first()
            _state.value = GuardUiState.Bound(cachedName ?: "已绑定")
            runCatching { api.deviceMe("Bearer $token") }
                .onSuccess {
                    GuardLog.i("auth", "token refresh ok")
                    preferences.saveBinding(token, it.childId, it.childName)
                    _state.value = GuardUiState.Bound(it.childName)
                }
                .onFailure { error ->
                    // 仅令牌失效时清除；网络异常保留本地绑定，离线可用。
                    if (error is HttpException && (error.code() == 401 || error.code() == 403)) {
                        GuardLog.w("auth", "token rejected HTTP ${error.code()}, re-activate")
                        preferences.clearBinding()
                        startActivation()
                    } else {
                        GuardLog.w("auth", "token refresh failed: ${error.message}")
                    }
                }
        }
    }

    private fun startActivation() {
        pollJob?.cancel()
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _state.value = GuardUiState.Loading

                v

Large rewrite. Need read QuizActivity and quiz data/API to see current quiz flow and data model, and build.gradle dependencies, manifest permissions, strings. Also question seed data in migrations.

User's new target:
- TV local data, install & use.
- No server, no activation, no parent management.
- All questions mixed, no age filter.
- Only one switch: enable scheduled quiz.
- "定时答题" likely periodic quiz while watching; needs define interval? Maybe fixed interval (e.g., every 30 min) and question count (e.g., 3 questions). "只有是否开启定时答题的开关" suggests just on/off, no interval setting; fixed defaults. Could be: when enabled, every 30 minutes of watching triggers a quiz. Off = no quiz.

We need decide defaults and behavior. Per rules, design + plan; and maybe ask clarifying questions because semantics matter:
1. When the switch is on, how often should the quiz pop up? Fixed (e.g., every 30 min) or at clock times (e.g., 20:00)? "定时答题" could mean scheduled at set times, but only a toggle means fixed schedule. Maybe they mean "定时弹出答题" = periodic.
2. What happens if answered correctly? Continue; wrong? Maybe still continue? Existing flow blocks on fail.
3. How many questions per session? 1? 3?
4. Does it guard only video apps or any app?
5. Local question data source: export from server DB. How many questions? Need inspect seeds. If no seed data (migrations create tables only), maybe only sample/seed in a migration. Let's check.

Let's inspect migrations for question seeds.

package com.openquizgo.guard.data.remote

import com.google.gson.annotations.SerializedName
import retrofit2.Response
import retrofit2.http.Body
import retrofit2.http.DELETE
import retrofit2.http.GET
import retrofit2.http.Header
import retrofit2.http.POST
import retrofit2.http.PUT
import retrofit2.http.Path

data class MetaResponse(
    @SerializedName("service") val service: String,
    @SerializedName("version") val version: String,
    @SerializedName("env") val env: String,
)

interface GuardApi {
    @GET("v1/meta")
    suspend fun meta(): MetaResponse

    @POST("v1/device/activate/code")
    suspend fun requestActivationCode(@Body request: ActivationCodeRequest): ActivationCodeResponse

    @POST("v1/device/token")
    suspend fun exchangeDeviceToken(@Body request: DeviceTokenRequest): Response<DeviceTokenResponse>

    @GET("v1/device/me")
    suspend fun deviceMe(@Header("Authorization") authorization: String): DeviceMeResponse

    @DELETE("v1/device/me")
    suspend fun unbindDeviceMe(@Header("Authorization") authorization: String): Response<Unit>

    @POST("v1/quiz/sessions")
    suspend fun createQuizSession(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
    ): Response<CreateSessionResponse>

    @GET("v1/quiz/sessions/{sessionID}")
    suspend fun quizSession(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Path("sessionID") sessionId: String,
    ): Response<SessionSummary>

    @POST("v1/quiz/sessions/{sessionID}/answers")
    suspend fun submitQuizAnswer(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Path("sessionID") sessionId: String,
        @Body request: AnswerSubmission,
    ): Response<AnswerResult>

    @POST("v1/quiz/sessions/{sessionID}/finish")
    suspend fun finishQuizSession(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Path("sessionID") sessionId: String,
    ): Response<SessionSummary>

    @GET("v1/watch/grants/active")
    suspend fun activeGrant(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
    ): Response<WatchGrant>

    @POST("v1/watch/grants/{grantID}/heartbeat")
    suspend fun heartbeat(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Path("grantID") grantId: String,
        @Body request: HeartbeatRequest,
    ): Response<HeartbeatResult>

    @GET("v1/offline/lease")
    suspend fun offlineLease(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
    ): Response<OfflineLease>

    @POST("v1/watch/logs")
    suspend fun reportOfflineUsage(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Body request: OfflineReportRequest,
    ): Response<OfflineReportResult>

    @PUT("v1/device/capability")
    suspend fun reportCapability(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Body request: CapabilityReport,
    ): Response<Unit>

    @POST("v1/device/tamper")
    suspend fun reportTamper(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Body request: TamperReport,
    ): Response<Unit>

    @POST("v1/device/diagnostics")
    suspend fun uploadDiagnostics(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Body request: DiagnosticUploadRequest,
    ): Response<Unit>

    @POST("v1/device/battles/join")
    suspend fun joinBattle(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Body request: BattleJoinRequest,
    ): Response<BattleState>

    @GET("v1/device/battles/{battleID}")
    suspend fun battleState(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Path("battleID") battleId: String,
    ): Response<BattleState>

    @POST("v1/device/battles/{battleID}/answers")
    suspend fun submitBattleAnswers(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Path("battleID") battleId: String,
        @Body request: BattleSubmitRequest,
    ): Response<BattleState>

    @POST("v1/device/unlock")
    suspend fun deviceUnlock(
        @Header("Authorization") authorization: String,
        @Body request: DeviceUnlockRequest,
    ): Response<WatchGrant>
}

data class BattleJoinRequest(
    @SerializedName("code") val code: String,
)

data class BattleSubmitRequest(
    @SerializedName("answers") val answers: List<BattleAnswer>,
)

data class BattleAnswer(
    @SerializedName("question_id") val questionId: String,
    @SerializedName("answer") val answer: List<String>,
)

data class BattleQuestion(
    @SerializedName("id") val id: String,
    @SerializedName("stem") val stem: String,
    @SerializedName("type") val type: String,
    @SerializedName("stem_media") val stemMedia: StemMedia? = null,
    @SerializedName("options") val options: List<QuizOption>? = null,
)

data class BattleResult(
    @SerializedName("winner") val winner: String?,
    @SerializedName("child_score") val childScore: Int?,
    @SerializedName("parent_score") val parentScore: Int?,
)

data class BattleState(
    @SerializedName("id") val id: String,
    @SerializedName("status") val status: String,
    @SerializedName("question_count") val questionCount: Int,
    @SerializedName("questions") val questions: List<BattleQuestion>? = null,
    @SerializedName("child_score") val childScore: Int = 0,
    @SerializedName("parent_score") val parentScore: Int = 0,
    @SerializedName("mode") val mode: String? = null,
    @SerializedName("result") val result: BattleResult? = null,
)

data class DeviceUnlockRequest(
    @SerializedName("pin") val pin: String,
    @SerializedName("minutes") val minutes: Int = 10,
)

data class DiagnosticUploadRequest(
    @SerializedName("content") val content: String,
    @SerializedName("note") val note: String = "",
)

data class ActivationCodeRequest(
    @SerializedName("device_id") val deviceId: String,
    @SerializedName("platform") val platform: String = "tv",
)

data class ActivationCodeResponse(
    @SerializedName("code") val code: String,
    @SerializedName("expires_in") val expiresIn: Int,
)

    @SerializedNa

-- ============================================================
-- 题目主表：唯一题库，学科只是标签。
-- ============================================================
create table question (
    id             uuid        primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
    subject_code   text        not null,
    category_code  text        not null,
    stage_code     text        not null,
    grade_tag      text,
    difficulty     smallint    not null,
    type_code      text        not null,
    stem           text        not null,
    stem_media     jsonb,
    options        jsonb,
    answer         jsonb       not null,
    answer_alias   jsonb,
    explain        text,
    duration_limit smallint,
    topic_id       uuid        references topic_pack (id) on delete set null,
    status         text        not null default 'draft',
    version        integer     not null default 1,
    schema_ver     integer     not null default 1,
    created_by     uuid        references admin_user (id) on delete set null,
    reviewed_by    uuid        references admin_user (id) on delete set null,
    created_at     timestamptz not null default now(),
    updated_at     timestamptz not null default now(),
    constraint question_stage_chk check (stage_code in ('preschool', 'low', 'mid', 'high')),
    constraint question_category_chk check (category_code in ('core', 'literacy', 'topic', 'fun')),
    constraint question_difficulty_chk check (difficulty between 1 and 5),
    constraint question_status_chk check (status in ('draft', 'review', 'trial', 'online', 'offline')),
    constraint question_type_chk check (type_code in ('choice', 'voice', 'image', 'fill', 'sort', 'match')),
    constraint question_grade_chk check (grade_tag is null or grade_tag ~ '^g[1-9]$'),
    constraint question_options_chk check (options is null or jsonb_typeof(options) = 'array'),
    constraint question_alias_chk check (category_code <> 'fun' or jsonb_array_length(coalesce(answer_alias, '[]'::jsonb)) > 0),
    constraint question_duration_chk check (duration_limit is null or duration_limit between 5 and 120),
    constraint question_version_chk check (version > 0 and schema_ver > 0)
);

comment on table question is '题目主表：学科/类目/学段/难度/题型均为标签，禁止按学科拆表';

create index question_online_pool_idx on question (stage_code, subject_code, difficulty) where status = 'online';
create index question_online_category_idx on question (category_code, stage_code) where status = 'online';
create index question_topic_idx on question (topic_id) where topic_id is not null;
create index question_review_idx on question (status, updated_at desc);

-- ============================================================
-- 题包与题目关联

create table topic_question (
    topic_id    uuid    not null references topic_pack (id) on delete cascade,
    question_id uuid    not null references question (id) on delete cascade,
    position    integer not null default 0,
    primary key (topic_id, question_id)
);

create index topic_question_question_idx on topic_question (question_id);
