import java.util.Properties

plugins {
    id("com.android.application")
    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.compose")
}

android {
    namespace = "com.openquizgo.guard"
    compileSdk = 35

    defaultConfig {
        applicationId = "com.openquizgo.quiz"
        minSdk = 26
        targetSdk = 33
        versionCode = 104
        versionName = "1.0.4"
        val apiBaseUrl = providers.gradleProperty("apiBaseUrl")
            .orElse("http://192.168.1.27:8080/")
            .get()
        buildConfigField("String", "API_BASE_URL", "\"$apiBaseUrl\"")
        val expectedSignature = providers.gradleProperty("expectedSignature")
            .orElse("")
            .get()
        buildConfigField("String", "EXPECTED_SIGNATURE", "\"$expectedSignature\"")
    }

    // 生产签名：在仓库根目录放置 keystore.properties（已 gitignore），否则回退 debug 签名仅供内部验收。
    val keystorePropsFile = rootProject.file("keystore.properties")
    val hasReleaseKey = keystorePropsFile.exists()
    val releaseProps = Properties().apply {
        if (hasReleaseKey) keystorePropsFile.inputStream().use { load(it) }
    }

    signingConfigs {
        if (hasReleaseKey) {
            create("release") {
                storeFile = rootProject.file(releaseProps.getProperty("storeFile"))
                storePassword = releaseProps.getProperty("storePassword")
                keyAlias = releaseProps.getProperty("keyAlias")
                keyPassword = releaseProps.getProperty("keyPassword")
            }
        }
    }

    buildTypes {
        release {
            isMinifyEnabled = false
            signingConfig = if (hasReleaseKey) {
                signingConfigs.getByName("release")
            } else {
                signingConfigs.getByName("debug")
            }
        }
    }

    buildFeatures {
        compose = true
        buildConfig = true
    }

    compileOptions {
        sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
        targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    }

    packaging {
        resources.excludes += "/META-INF/{AL2.0,LGPL2.1}"
    }

    testOptions {
        // GuardLog 直接调用 android.util.Log；JVM 单测下返回默认值即可。
        unitTests.isReturnDefaultValues = true
    }
}

<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    xmlns:tools="http://schemas.android.com/tools">

    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED" />
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.PACKAGE_USAGE_STATS"
        tools:ignore="ProtectedPermissions" />

    <uses-feature
        android:name="android.software.leanback"
        android:required="true" />
    <uses-feature
        android:name="android.hardware.touchscreen"
        android:required="false" />

    <application
        android:name=".GuardApplication"
        android:allowBackup="false"
        android:label="OpenQuizGo 守护端"
        android:supportsRtl="true"
        android:theme="@style/Theme.Guard">

        <activity
            android:name=".ui.MainActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:screenOrientation="landscape">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN" />
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.LEANBACK_LAUNCHER" />
            </intent-filter>
        </activity>

        <activity
            android:name=".ui.QuizActivity"
            android:exported="false"
            android:launchMode="singleTask"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.Guard.Translucent" />

        <activity
            android:name=".ui.BattleActivity"
            android:exported="false"
            android:launchMode="singleTask" />

        <activity
            android:name=".ui.BlockerActivity"
            android:exported="false"
            android:launchMode="singleTask"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.Guard" />

        <service
            android:name=".platform.GuardService"

```text
package question

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"time"
)

// Question 是题目的管理端完整视图。
type Question struct {
	ID            string          `json:"id"`
	Subject       string          `json:"subject"`
	Category      string          `json:"category"`
	Stage         string          `json:"stage"`
	GradeTag      string          `json:"grade_tag,omitempty"`
	Difficulty    int             `json:"difficulty"`
	Type          string          `json:"type"`
	Stem          string          `json:"stem"`
	StemMedia     json.RawMessage `json:"stem_media,omitempty"`
	Options       json.RawMessage `json:"options,omitempty"`
	Answer        json.RawMessage `json:"answer"`
	AnswerAlias   json.RawMessage `json:"answer_alias,omitempty"`
	Explain       string          `json:"explain,omitempty"`
	DurationLimit *int            `json:"duration_limit,omitempty"`
	TopicID       string          `json:"topic_id,omitempty"`
	Status        string          `json:"status"`
	Version       int             `json:"version"`
	SchemaVer     int             `json:"schema_ver"`
	CreatedAt     time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt     time.Time       `json:"updated_at"`
}

// Input 是创建/更新题目的入参。
type Input struct {
	Subject       string          `json:"subject"`
	Category      string          `json:"category"`
	Stage         string          `json:"stage"`
	GradeTag      string          `json:"grade_tag"`
	Difficulty    int             `json:"difficulty"`
	Type          string          `json:"type"`
	Stem          string          `json:"stem"`
	StemMedia     json.RawMessage `json:"stem_media"`
	Options       json.RawMessage `json:"options"`
	Answer        json.RawMessage `json:"answer"`
	AnswerAlias   json.RawMessage `json:"answer_alias"`
	Explain       string          `json:"explain"`
	DurationLimit *int            `json:"duration_limit"`
	TopicID       *string         `json:"topic_id"`
	SchemaVer     int             `json:"schema_ver"`
}

// CoverageCell 是题库覆盖率聚合单元。
type CoverageCell struct {
	Subject    string `json:"subject"`
	Stage      string `json:"stage"`
	Type       string `json:"type"`
	Status     string `json:"status"`
	Difficulty int    `json:"difficulty"`
	Count      int    `json:"count"`
}

// CoverageSnapshot 是某一天的题库覆盖快照。
type CoverageSnapshot struct {
	CapturedOn string `json:"captured_on"`
	Total      int    `json:"total"`
	Online     int    `json:"online"`
	Draft      int    `json:"draft"`
	InReview   int    `json:"in_review"`
	Offline    int    `json:"offline"`
}

// CoverageResult 是覆盖率看板数据。
type CoverageResult struct {
	Cells    []CoverageCell `json:"cells"`
	Total    int            `json:"total"`
	Warnings []string       `json:"warnings"`
}

// TopicCoverageCell 是专题维度覆盖率聚合单元。
// topic_id 为空表示未加入任何专题，topic_name 固定为「未分专题」。
type TopicCoverageCell struct {
	TopicID   string `json:"topic_id"`
	TopicName string `json:"topic_name"`
	Status    string `json:"status"`
	Count     int    `json:"count"`
}

// TopicCoverageResult 是专题覆盖率看板数据；Total 为去重后的题目总量。
type TopicCoverageResult struct {
	Cells    []TopicCoverageCell `json:"cells"`
	Total    int                 `json:"total"`
	Warnings []string            `json:"warnings"`
}

// CalibrationItem 是难度校准建议。
type CalibrationItem struct {
	QuestionID          string  `json:"question_id"`
	Stem                string  `json:"stem"`
	Subject             string  `json:"subject"`
	Stage               string  `json:"stage"`
	Difficulty          int     `json:"difficulty"`
	Answered            int     `json:"answered"`
	Correct             int     `json:"correct"`
	Accuracy            float64 `json:"accuracy"`
	SuggestedDifficulty int     `json:"suggested_difficulty"`
	Reason              string  `json:"reason"`
}

// CalibrationThresholds 是校准阈值。
type CalibrationThresholds struct {
	MinAnswers int     `json:"min_answers"`
	Up         float64 `json:"up"`
	Down       float64 `json:"down"`
}

// CalibrationResult 是校准预览结果。
type CalibrationResult struct {
	Items      []CalibrationItem     `json:"items"`
	Thresholds CalibrationThresholds `json:"thresholds"`
}

```text
package quiz

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"math"
	"math/rand"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"

	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/rule"
	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/internal/watch"
)

// Rule 是规则子集别名，统一由 rule 包校验。
type Rule = rule.Rule

const defaultPoolLimit = 2000

var (
	stageRanks = map[string]int{"preschool": 0, "low": 1, "mid": 2, "high": 3}
	stageOrder = map[string][]string{
		"preschool": {"preschool", "low", "mid", "high"},
		"low":       {"low", "preschool", "mid", "high"},
		"mid":       {"mid", "low", "high", "preschool"},
		"high":      {"high", "mid", "low", "preschool"},
	}
	validStages = map[string]bool{"preschool": true, "low": true, "mid": true, "high": true}
)

// Service 是组卷服务。
type Service struct {
	repo     *repository
	redis    *redis.Client
	ruleSvc  *rule.Service
	watchSvc *watch.Service
	logger   *slog.Logger
}

// New 构造组卷服务。
func New(pool *pgxpool.Pool, rdb *redis.Client, ruleSvc *rule.Service, watchSvc *watch.Service, logger *slog.Logger) *Service {
	return &Service{
		repo:     newRepository(pool),
		redis:    rdb,
		ruleSvc:  ruleSvc,
		watchSvc: watchSvc,
		logger:   logger,
	}
}

// Select 按规则组卷。
func (s *Service) Select(ctx context.Context, req SelectRequest) (Result, error) {
	stage, grade := req.Stage, req.GradeTag
	if req.ChildID != "" {
		childStage, childGrade, err := s.repo.childStage(ctx, req.ChildID)
		if err != nil {
			return Result{}, newError(404, "CHILD_NOT_FOUND", "孩子档案不存在")
		}
		stage, grade = childStage, childGrade
	}
	stage = strings.TrimSpace(stage)
	if !validStages[stage] {
		return Result{}, newError(422, "VALIDATION_ERROR", "学段不合法")
	}
	if err := rule.Validate(req.Rule); err != nil {
		return Result{}, newError(422, "VALIDATION_ERROR", err.Error())
	}

	seed := req.Seed
	if seed == 0 {
		seed = time.Now().UnixNano()
	}
	rng := rand.New(rand.NewSource(seed))

	stats, err := s.repo.difficultyStats(ctx, req.ChildID)
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, err
	}
	target := targetDifficulty(stage, grade, stats)

	n, err := rule.SessionSize(req.Rule)
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, newError(422, "VALIDATION_ERROR", err.Error())
	}
	if req.MaxQuestions > 0 && req.MaxQuestions < n {
		n = max(3, req.MaxQuestions)
	}

	subjectWeights := make(map[string]float64)
	for subject, weight := range req.Rule.SubjectWeight {
		if subject == "fun" || weight <= 0 {
			continue
		}
		subjectWeights[subject] = weight
	}
	if len(subjectWeights) == 0 {
		return Result{}, newError(422, "VALIDATION_ERROR", "至少需要一个学科权重")
	}

	selected := make([]Selected, 0, n)
	chosen := map[string]bool{}
	warnings := make([]string, 0)
	addWarning := func(w string) {
		for _, existing := range warnings {
			if existing == w {
				return
			}
		}
		warnings = append(warnings, w)
	}

	stages := stageOrder[stage]
	primaryRank := stageRanks[stage]

	subjectKeys := make([]string, 0, len(subjectWeights))

```text
package com.openquizgo.guard.platform

import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityService
import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityServiceInfo
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.view.KeyEvent
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityEvent
import com.openquizgo.guard.ui.QuizActivity
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

object ForegroundTracker {
    private val _packageName = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val packageName: StateFlow<String?> = _packageName.asStateFlow()

    fun update(pkg: CharSequence?) {
        val value = pkg?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
        if (value.isNotEmpty() && value != _packageName.value) {
            _packageName.value = value
        }
    }
}

class GuardAccessibilityService : AccessibilityService() {

    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    @Volatile
    private var quizArmed = false

    override fun onServiceConnected() {
        super.onServiceConnected()
        instance = this
        getServiceInfo()?.let { info ->
            info.flags = info.flags or AccessibilityServiceInfo.FLAG_REQUEST_FILTER_KEY_EVENTS
            setServiceInfo(info)
        }
        GuardLog.i("accessibility", "connected")
    }

    override fun onKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
        if (event.action == KeyEvent.ACTION_UP && event.keyCode == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_CENTER) {
            GuardLog.i("confirm key, armed=$quizArmed")
            if (quizArmed) {
                quizArmed = false
                handler.postDelayed({ launchQuiz() }, 500)
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun launchQuiz() {
        val intent = Intent(this, QuizActivity::class.java)
            .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP)
        runCatching { startActivity(intent) }
            .onSuccess { GuardLog.i("accessibility", "quiz activity launched") }
            .onFailure { GuardLog.e("accessibility", "quiz launch failed", it) }
    }

    fun armQuiz() {
        quizArmed = true
    }

    fun disarmQuiz() {
        quizArmed = false
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        instance = null
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {
        if (event?.eventType != AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED) return
        val pkg = event.packageName ?: return
        ForegroundTracker.update(pkg)
        GuardLog.i("foreground=$pkg cls=${event.className}")
    }

    override fun onInterrupt() {
        GuardLog.w("accessibility", "interrupted")
    }

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var instance: GuardAccessibilityService? = null

        fun armQuiz() {
            instance?.armQuiz()
        }

        fun disarmQuiz() {
            instance?.disarmQuiz()
        }

        fun isConnected(): Boolean = instance != null
    }
}
===== FOREGROUND =====
package com.openquizgo.guard.data.foreground

import android.app.usage.UsageEvents
import android.app.usage.UsageStatsManager
import android.content.Context
import android.media.AudioManager
import com.openquizgo.guard.platform.ForegroundTracker
import com.openquizgo.guard.platform.GuardLog
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class ForegroundState(
    val packageName: String? = null,
    val mediaActive: Boolean = false,
    val mediaSeconds: Long = 0,
)

/**
 * 前台识别：无障碍事件为主，UsageStats 每 5s 校正；媒体播放以 AudioManager 判断。
 */
class ForegroundDetector(
    context: Context,
    scope: CoroutineScope,
) : com.openquizgo.guard.domain.ForegroundSource {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext
    private val usageStats = appContext.getSystemService(Context.USAGE_STATS_SERVICE) as? UsageStatsManager
    private val audioManager = appContext.getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as? AudioManager

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(ForegroundState())
    override val state: StateFlow<ForegroundState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    init {
        scope.launch {
            ForegroundTracker.packageName.collect { pkg ->
                _state.update { it.copy(packageName = pkg ?: it.packageName) }
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(5_000)
                refreshFromUsageStats()
                val active = audioManager?.isMusicActive == true
                if (active != _state.value.mediaActive) {
                    GuardLog.i("mediaActive=$active pkg=${_state.value.packageName}")
                }
                _state.update { it.copy(mediaActive = active) }
            }
        }
        scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                delay(1_000)
                val current = _state.value
                if (current.mediaActive) {
                    _state.update { it.copy(mediaSeconds = it.mediaSeconds + 1) }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun refreshFromUsageStats() {
        val manager = usageStats ?: return
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val events: UsageEvents = manager.queryEvents(now - 10_000, now)
        var lastPackage: String? = null
        val event = UsageEvents.Event()
        while (events.hasNextEvent()) {
            events.getNextEvent(event)
            if (event.eventType == UsageEvents.Event.MOVE_TO_FOREGROUND) {
                lastPackage = event.packageName
            }
        }
        if (!lastPackage.isNullOrBlank() && lastPackage != _state.value.packageName) {
            GuardLog.i("usageStats foreground=$lastPackage")

                    loading = false

@Composable
private fun QuestionScreen(
    question: SessionQuestion,
    index: Int,
    total: Int,
    result: AnswerResult?,
    submitting: Boolean,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    onNext: () -> Unit,
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var audioPlayer by remember { mutableStateOf<MediaPlayer?>(null) }

    fun playAudio(path: String) {
        audioPlayer?.release()
        audioPlayer = MediaPlayer().apply {
            try {
                setDataSource(resolveMediaUrl(path))
                setOnPreparedListener { it.start() }
                setOnErrorListener { _, _, _ -> true }
                prepareAsync()
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                release()
                audioPlayer = null
            }
        }
    }

    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose {
            audioPlayer?.release()
            audioPlayer = null
        }
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color(0xFF0B0B0B))
            .padding(64.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
    ) {
        Text(
            text = "答题时间 · 第 $index / $total 题",
            fontSize = 26.sp,
            color = Color(0xFFB0B0B0),
        )
        Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
        Text(
            text = question.stem,
            fontSize = 44.sp,

The existing app can start QuizActivity from accessibility service when armed (user presses OK). For automatic popup, we can call `QuizActivity` start directly from service; background activity launch restrictions might block. But targetSdk 33 and app has a foreground service (GuardService) — background activity launch still restricted. However, the existing `watchdogTick` calls `launchQuiz()` from service after 90s fallback; it may work on TVs due to lenient OEM/older Android? The app's target device is Android TV (Skyworth). Min SDK 26, target 33.

Safer approach: keep the "按确认键开始答题" prompt: when 15 min elapse, show a Toast/prompt and arm the accessibility service; if the user doesn't press OK within e.g. 10 seconds, auto-launch (fallback). For an unattended kid, they must press OK to continue watching; but they could ignore and keep watching. To enforce pause, we need to actually pause media. The app can't pause a third-party player directly except by launching an activity (losing focus) or using accessibility media controls (dispatchMediaKeyEvent pause). The design 002 uses full-screen Activity to make player lose focus and stop; that's the proven approach. So auto-launching QuizActivity at 15 min is right.

Can a foreground service start an activity? Android 10+ restricts unless the app has SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW or is an accessibility service. Our app runs an AccessibilityService; starting an activity from the accessibility service context might be exempt? The exemption list includes "app has a service bound by the system" (accessibility service) — yes, accessibility services are exempt from background activity launch restrictions I believe. The existing code starts QuizActivity from `GuardAccessibilityService` (a bound service) successfully. So trigger the launch from the accessibility service, not the GuardService. Add a method `launchQuizNow()` to GuardAccessibilityService, call it when timer reaches 15 min. That should work.

Alternatively use a full-screen notification intent, but that's heavier.

So architecture:
- `LocalGuardEngine` tracks media active + enabled, timer 900s.
- On reaching threshold, call `GuardAccessibilityService.launchQuizNow()` (or service `launchQuiz()` if accessibility disconnected; fallback toast).
- QuizActivity local mode: question bank asset; one question; options. On wrong: show "答错了，再试一次" and keep the same question with wrong option marked/disabled? "暂停播放，直到答对" → since QuizActivity is full-screen, playback is paused. Allow retry until correct. On correct: `engine.onPassed()`, show "回答正确" and "继续观看" button; finish.
- If the user presses Back, should we block? To enforce pause until correct, QuizActivity should intercept back and not finish. Existing BlockerActivity does. QuizActivity currently allows back → onQuizCancelled. In local mode, intercept Back so they can't escape. Also onPause? If they press Home, app goes background and playback might resume. Need watchdog: GuardService checks if state QUIZ_REQUIRED and quiz activity not showing → relaunch. Keep watchdog logic.
- Toggle off: if turned off while quiz showing? Probably let current quiz finish; or dismiss. The home toggle is set by an adult; if off, no future quizzes. If a quiz is currently showing and toggle changes, hard to access. On/off only from MainActivity.

- Permissions: accessibility + usage stats. Usage stats for foreground/media? Media detection uses AudioManager (isMusicActive), which works without usage stats; foreground package used to only trigger for non-app media? For local mode, we only need mediaActive (any audio playing, including TV apps). Does `isMusicActive` detect TV video? On Android TV, media playback via AudioManager should report true. Existing code uses it. We can simply trigger on mediaActive regardless of package. That avoids UsageStats entirely, requiring only accessibility service for launching the quiz. But media detection may be false for some players; existing fallback uses known media packages via foreground. We can keep UsageStats permission optional.
  - Simplify: trigger whenever `mediaActive` true, accumulate. No package filtering. This works for any video app.
  - Accessibility is needed to auto-launch QuizActivity; but we could launch from GuardService directly, possibly blocked. Keep accessibility required.
- Install-and-use: still need to enable accessibility service manually (Android requires). First-run home screen guides with a big button to open accessibility settings and usage settings. "电视安装上就能用" can't be literally zero-config because Android TV requires accessibility permission for overlay; but we can minimize.
- MainActivity toggle: default ON. Use DataStore.
- Remove binding/activation gate; home shows directly.
- No server calls. Can we remove Retrofit/OkHttp dependencies? If we delete server files, yes. But keeping them compiles. For APK size, remove later.
- Battle/daily/recommendations gone from TV UI.

**Scope decision**: Rewrite core runtime and UI, delete or leave dormant:

2. Leave old server classes in place but unreferenced. They compile, APK includes them and network permissions remain. Functionally local. This minimizes deletion risk and satisfies "temporarily no parent management". Later cleanup.

package com.openquizgo.guard.platform

import android.app.ActivityManager
import android.app.AppOpsManager
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.Settings
import android.os.Process

object Permissions {

    fun accessibilityEnabled(context: Context): Boolean {
        val enabled = Settings.Secure.getString(
            context.contentResolver,
            Settings.Secure.ENABLED_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES,
        )
        return enabled != null &&
            enabled.contains(context.packageName) &&
            enabled.contains("GuardAccessibilityService")
    }

    fun usageAccessEnabled(context: Context): Boolean {
        val appOps = context.getSystemService(Context.APP_OPS_SERVICE) as? AppOpsManager ?: return false
        val mode = appOps.checkOpNoThrow(
            AppOpsManager.OPSTR_GET_USAGE_STATS,
            Process.myUid(),
            context.packageName,
        )
        return mode == AppOpsManager.MODE_ALLOWED
    }

    fun notificationsEnabled(context: Context): Boolean {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.N) return true
        val manager = context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as? NotificationManager
            ?: return false
        return manager.areNotificationsEnabled()
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    fun foregroundServiceRunning(context: Context): Boolean {
        val manager = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as? ActivityManager
            ?: return false
        return manager.getRunningServices(Int.MAX_VALUE)
            .any { it.service.className == GuardService::class.java.name }
    }

    fun accessibilitySettingsIntent() = android.content.Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS)

    fun usageSettingsIntent() = android.content.Intent(Settings.ACTION_USAGE_ACCESS_SETTINGS)
}
===
package com.openquizgo.guard.platform

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent

class BootReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {
    override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent?) {
        when (intent?.action) {
            Intent.ACTION_BOOT_COMPLETED,
            Intent.ACTION_MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED -> {
                GuardLog.i("boot", "boot/replace -> start guard service")
                runCatching { GuardService.start(context) }
                    .onFailure { GuardLog.e("boot", "start guard service failed", it) }
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.openquizgo.guard.platform

import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone

/**
 * 应用私有目录下的滚动日志文件（049）。
 *
 * - 写入 `files/logs/guard.log`，单文件上限 [MAX_BYTES]（1MB）；
 * - 超出后轮转为 `guard.log.1`，只保留一份备份；
 * - 所有入口线程安全，任何 IO/权限异常都会被吞掉，不影响主流程。
 *
 * 不依赖 Android API，便于在 JVM 单元测试中使用临时目录验证。
 */
class LogStore(
    private val logDir: File,
    private val maxBytes: Long = MAX_BYTES,
) {
    private val logFile = File(logDir, FILE_NAME)
    private val rotatedFile = File(logDir, ROTATED_FILE_NAME)
    private val timeFormat = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS", Locale.US).apply {
        timeZone = TimeZone.getDefault()
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun write(level: String, tag: String, message: String) {
        runCatching {
            val bytes = buildLine(level, tag, message).toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
            val payload = if (bytes.size > maxBytes) {
                bytes.copyOf(maxBytes.coerceAtMost(Int.MAX_VALUE.toLong()).toInt())
            } else {
                bytes
            }
            if (!logDir.exists()) logDir.mkdirs()
            if (logFile.length() > 0 && logFile.length() + payload.size > maxBytes) {
                rotate()
            }
            FileOutputStream(logFile, true).use { it.write(payload) }
        }
    }

    private fun buildLine(level: String, tag: String, message: String): String {
        val safeTag = tag.replace('\n', ' ').replace('\r', ' ').take(MAX_TAG_CHARS)
        val safeMessage = message.replace('\n', ' ').replace('\r', ' ').take(MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS)
        return "${timeFormat.format(Date())} $level/$safeTag $safeMessage\n"
    }

    private fun rotate() {
        if (rotatedFile.exists()) rotatedFile.delete()
        if (!logFile.renameTo(rotatedFile)) {
            // 某些文件系统不允许 rename 覆盖，退化为复制后清空当前文件。
            runCatching {
                logFile.copyTo(rotatedFile, overwrite = true)
                logFile.delete()
            }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val DIRECTORY = "logs"
        const val FILE_NAME = "guard.log"
        const val ROTATED_FILE_NAME = "guard.log.1"
        const val MAX_BYTES = 1024L * 1024L

        private const val MAX_TAG_CHARS = 64
        private const val MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS = 8 * 1024
    }
}

```

Design 077 key sections:
- Background: parent app paused; server/domain not available; TV must work standalone locally.
- Goals:
  - Install & use without server/account/parent app.
  - Bundled local question bank, all stages mixed, no grade filter.
  - Only setting: 定时答题 on/off (default on).
  - Every 15 minutes of actual media playback, one single-choice question; correct → continue; wrong → stay until correct.
  - Permissions: accessibility required; usage stats optional/no.
- Non-goals: parent management, binding, member, battle, cloud sync, online question updates, multiple profiles.
- Architecture:
  - LocalQuestionBank (assets/questions.json)
  - LocalGuardSettings (DataStore)
  - LocalGuardEngine (mediaActive accumulation, state machine)
  - GuardService (foreground notification, watchdog)
  - GuardAccessibilityService (auto-launch quiz; key fallback)
  - MainActivity (toggle + permissions + status)
  - QuizActivity (1 question, retry until correct, back blocked)
  - Remove server modules.
- Data model: JSON schema.
- Interface: internal Kotlin APIs.
- Non-functional: 15-min timer accuracy ±5s, battery, offline, APK size.
- Risks: background activity launch restrictions → launch via accessibility service + watchdog; TV players may not report mediaActive → fallback to foreground app/heuristic; wrong answer lockout; question quality.
- Acceptance: no network dependency; airplane mode works; 15 min trigger; toggle off; wrong answer blocks; correct resumes; reboot restarts; tests/build.

TV 端完全离线可用：安装后不需要服务器、账号、家长端或网络。\n2. 内置本地题库（≥100 道单选题），不按学龄/年级筛选，所有题目混合随机出题。\n3. 首页只有一个业务开关：**定时答题**，默认开启。\n4. 定时规则：累计视频播放满 **15 分钟** 自动弹出 1 道单选题；暂停播放、退出视频不计时。\n5. 答对后继续观看；答错则保持答题界面，**直到答对**才可退出。\n6. 关闭开关后不再弹出答题；重新开启后重新累计 15 分钟。\n7. 重启电视/开机自启后开关状态保留，守护服务自动恢复。\n\n### 1.3 非目标（明确不做什么）\n\n- 家长端绑定、激活码、远程解锁、家庭 PIN；\n- 服务端题库、答题会话、授权与心跳、离线租约、对战、会员；\n- 学龄/年级/难度筛选、错题本、学习报告、数据上传；\n- 在线更新题库（本期题库随 APK 打包，后续通过新版本替换）；\n- iOS/Android 手机端。\n\n## 2. 需求与验收标准\n\n| 编号 | 需求 | 验收标准 | 优先级 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| R1 | 离线可用 | 飞行模式、无网络、无服务器时，安装后即可进入首页并正常答题 | P0 |\n| R2 | 本地题库 | APK 内置 `assets/questions.json`，≥100 道题，含题干、4 个选项、正确答案、解析 | P0 |\n| R3 | 混合出题 | 出题不读取学龄/年级，所有题目等概率随机；连续两轮不重复同一题 | P0 |\n| R4 | 15 分钟计时 | 仅媒体播放时累计，满 900 秒触发；暂停/退出不累计 | P0 |\n| R5 | 开关 | 首页提供「定时答题」开关，默认开启；关闭后不触发 | P0 |\n| R6 | 答对继续 | 答对显示正确反馈，点击「继续观看」退出答题并重新计时 | P0 |\n| R7 | 答错锁定 | 答错停留原题，返回键无效，直到答对 | P0 |\n| R8 | 自动弹出 | 到点后自动全屏弹出答题界面；被切走/关闭时守护服务自动重新拉起 | P0 |\n| R9 | 开机自启 | 重启后服务恢复，开关与计时状态合理恢复 | P1 |\n| R10 | 质量门禁 | 单元测试通过，Debug/Release 构建通过，真机/模拟器可验收 | P0 |\n\n## 3. 方案对比与选型\n\n| 方案 | 优点 | 缺点 | 结论 |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| A. 保留服务端模式，增加离线缓存 | 改动小 | 仍需激活与服务器，违背本期目标 | 否决 |\n| B. 新增「本地模式」分支，保留服务端代码 | 可随时切回 | 两套状态机并存，复杂且易误触网络 | 否决 |\n| C. 删除服务端耦合，TV 改为纯本地单机应用 | 架构最简单，安装即用 | 需要重写状态机/答题界面并清理旧模块 | **采用** |\n| D. 单独新建 TV 工程 | 隔离彻底 | 复制权限/无障碍/媒体检测等成熟代码，维护两份 | 否决 |\n\n选型理由：当前阶段产品边界已经收敛为「本地题库 + 定时答题」，方案 C 可以显著降低运行复杂度和故障面；旧服务端代码保留在 Git 历史中，后续需要时再恢复。\n\n## 4. 总体架构\n\n### 4.1 模块划分\n\n| 模块 | 职责 |\n| --- | --- |\n| `data/local/LocalSettings` | DataStore 保存「定时答题」开关，默认开启 |\n| `data/local/QuestionBank` | 读取并解析 `assets/questions.json`，随机取题 |\n| `data/foreground/MediaPlaybackDetector` | 通过 `AudioManager` 判断媒体是否在播放 |\n| `domain/LocalGuardEngine` | 计时与状态机：关闭/观看/待答题/答题中/通过 |\n| `platform/GuardService` | 前台服务、通知、看门狗、到点拉起答题 |\n| `platform/GuardAccessibilityService` | 无障碍事件 + 自动拉起答题（后台启动 Activity 的可靠通道） |\n| `ui/MainActivity` | 首页：开关、状态、倒计时、权限入口 |\n| `ui/QuizActivity` | 单题答题、答错重试、答对退出 |\n\n### 4.2 状态机\n\n```text\n           开关关闭\n  ┌──────────────────────┐\n  ▼                      │\nPAUSED ──开关开启──> WATCHING ──播放累计 900s──> QUIZ_REQUIRED\n                         ▲                          │\n                         │                     拉起答题界面\n                         │                          ▼\n                         └────答对/重置计时──── QUIZZING\n                                                    │\n                                              答错：停留本题\n```\n\n- `PAUSED`：开关关闭，不计时、不弹题；\n- `WATCHING`：开关开启且媒体播放中，按秒累计；\n- `QUIZ_REQUIRED`：累计满 900 秒，等待答题界面展示；\n- `QUIZZING`：答题界面已展示，计时暂停；\n- 答对后回到 `WATCHING` 并把累计秒数清零；答错保持 `QUIZZING`。\n\n### 4.3 触发流程\n\n```text\nMediaPlaybackDetector(mediaActive=true)\n        │ 每秒\n        ▼\nLocalGuardEngine 累计 +1s\n        │ ≥900s\n        ▼\nQUIZ_REQUIRED\n        │\nGuardService 观察到状态 → GuardAccessibilityService.launchQuiz()\n        │\n        ▼\nQuizActivity（全屏，播放器失焦暂停）\n        │\n   答对 ──> engine.onPassed() ──> 计时清零 ──> 退出答题\n        │\n   答错 ──> 停留本题，返回键无效\n```\n\n看门狗每 2 秒检查：若状态为 `QUIZ_REQUIRED`/`QUIZZING` 且答题界面不在前台，则重新拉起。\n\n### 4.4 断点与常量\n\n| 常量 | 值 | 说明 |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `QUIZ_INTERVAL_SECONDS` | 900 | 每累计播放 15 分钟出 1 题 |\n| `QUESTIONS_PER_ROUND` | 1 | 每轮 1 题 |\n| `WATCHDOG_INTERVAL_MS` | 2000 | 答题界面丢失时重新拉起 |\n| `RELATIVE_RETRY` | 无 | 答错只重试当前题，不换题 |\n\n## 5. 数据模型\n\n### 5.1 本地题库 JSON\n\n`tv/app/src/main/assets/questions.json`：\n\n```json\n{\n class QuestionBank(private val questions: List<LocalQuestion>) {
  val size get() = questions.size
  fun random(excludeId: String? = null): LocalQuestion? {
    if (questions.isEmpty()) return null
    if (questions.size == 1) return questions.first()
    val pool = if (excludeId == null) questions else questions.filter { it.id != excludeId }
    return pool.random()
  }
  companion object {
    fun load(context: Context): QuestionBank { ... parse assets/questions.json ... }
  }
}

### 4. LocalGuardEngine

```kotlin
enum class LocalGuardState { PAUSED, WATCHING, QUIZ_REQUIRED, QUIZZING, GRANTED }

class LocalGuardEngine(
  private val settings: LocalSettings,
  private val playback: PlaybackSource,
  private val scope: CoroutineScope,
  private val intervalSeconds: Long = 900,
  private val clock: () -> Long = System::currentTimeMillis,
) {
  private val _state = MutableStateFlow(LocalGuardState.PAUSED)
  val state: StateFlow<LocalGuardState>
  private val _elapsedSeconds = MutableStateFlow(0L)
  val elapsedSeconds...
  val remainingSeconds: StateFlow<Long> = combine...
  private var enabled = true
  private var initialized = false

  init {
    scope.launch {
      val snapshot = settings.snapshot()
      enabled = snapshot.enabled
      _elapsed.value = snapshot.elapsed
      if (enabled) transition(WATCHING/PAUSED based on playback?) else PAUSED
      // persist loop every 5s/10s
    }
    scope.launch { settings.quizEnabled.collect { ... } }
    scope.launch { ticker }
    scope.launch { persist every 10s if changed }
  }
}
```
Careful with initialization race. Simpler:
- One init coroutine loads snapshot first, sets `ready=true`, then starts collectors? Use `scope.launch { restore(); collectors }`.
- Testability: allow injecting flows? Constructor takes `quizEnabled: Flow<Boolean>`? For tests, a fake settings interface is better:
```kotlin
interface LocalSettingsStore {
  val quizEnabled: Flow<Boolean>
  suspend fun snapshot(): LocalSnapshot
  suspend fun saveEnabled(value: Boolean)
  suspend fun saveElapsed(seconds: Long)
  suspend fun saveLastQuestionId(id: String?)
}
```
`LocalSettings` implements. Engine depends on interface. Tests fake.
- Playback source interface:
```kotlin
interface PlaybackSource { val mediaActive: StateFlow<Boolean> }
```
`MediaPlaybackDetector` exposes `mediaActive`.
- Engine ticker:
```kotlin
scope.launch {
  while (isActive) {
    delay(1000)
    if (!ready) continue
    if (!enabled) { if (state != PAUSED) transition(PAUSED); continue }
    if (state == QUIZZING || state == QUIZ_REQUIRED) continue
    if (playback.mediaActive.value) {
      val next = _elapsed.value + 1
      _elapsed.value = next
      transition(WATCHING)
      if (next >= interval) {
        transition(QUIZ_REQUIRED)
        // emit event
      }
    } else {
      transition(PAUSED? or WATCHING?) 
    }
  }
}
```
State semantics: `WATCHING` when media playing; when no media and enabled, maybe `PAUSED`? But PAUSED is also switch-off. Add `IDLE` state (enabled but not playing) vs PAUSED (disabled). Design says PAUSED = switch off; WATCHING = playing. Use `IDLE` for enabled + not playing. Update design? It currently lists PAUSED/WATCHING/... no IDLE. Could treat WATCHING as enabled regardless of media, with elapsed static; MainActivity shows "等待播放". But label WATCHING when not playing is wrong. Add `IDLE` enum and update design? Simpler: state `READY`? Let's add `IDLE` and update design state diagram minimally. Or keep state values: `DISABLED`, `WAITING`, `WATCHING`, `QUIZ_REQUIRED`, `QUIZZING`, `GRANTED`. Design uses PAUSED; rename to PAUSED for disabled, add WAITING for enabled idle. Update doc after.
  - Actually, PAUSED can mean not timing (either off or no playback). But toggle off vs idle distinction needed for UI? Home can use `enabled` separately. Engine can expose `enabled` StateFlow. Then state can be:
    - `IDLE`: timer not running (switch off or no media)
    - `WATCHING`: media playing, counting
    - `QUIZ_REQUIRED`, `QUIZZING`, `GRANTED`.
  - MainActivity shows enabled from settings/engine; state for status.
  - Simpler. Update design state diagram later.
- Trigger event: set state QUIZ_REQUIRED. GuardService collects.
- On quiz started: state QUIZZING. Persist elapsed=interval.
- On passed: elapsed=0, lastQuestionId set by QuizActivity, state GRANTED then next tick WATCHING/IDLE.
- On cancelled/back (shouldn't happen): state QUIZ_REQUIRED.
- setEnabled(false): enabled=false, state IDLE, persist; if QUIZ_REQUIRED/QUIZZING? Maybe leave quiz; MainActivity toggle can't be accessed during quiz anyway.
- setEnabled(true): if state IDLE/PAUSED, state WATCHING/IDLE based on playback.

Persist elapsed every 10s or on state change. If app killed, restore elapsed; if elapsed >= interval, start QUIZ_REQUIRED? On restore, if enabled and elapsed>=900, trigger quiz. Good.

For tests, fake clock/delay? Use `kotlinx-coroutines-test` with `runTest` and inject a ticker interval? Engine ticker uses delay(1000); `advanceTimeBy` works with test dispatcher. But `scope` passed must be test scope. Use `TestScope`. Add constructor param `tickMillis=1000L`, `intervalSeconds`. Tests with `runTest { val engine = LocalGuardEngine(..., backgroundScope, interval=3, tickMillis=1000); ... advanceTimeBy(...) }`. Need flows.

- `remainingSeconds`: a StateFlow updated each tick based on enabled/state; compute `(interval - elapsed).coerceAtLeast(0)`. For idle/no media, show remaining unchanged. `combine` can be used but easiest `_remaining` updated in tick.
- Expose `enabled` StateFlow to UI.

```kotlin
class QuizActivity : ComponentActivity() {
  engine, bank, settings

}

```kotlin
class QuestionBank private constructor(val questions: List<LocalQuestion>) {
  val size get() = questions.size
  fun random(excludeId: String?): LocalQuestion? {
    if (questions.isEmpty()) return null
    val pool = if (excludeId == null || questions.size == 1) questions else questions.filter { it.id != excludeId }
    return pool.random()
  }
  companion object {
    fun load(context: Context): QuestionBank { ... }
    fun fromJson(text: String): QuestionBank { ... } // testable
  }
}
```
`fromJson` can be internal/public for tests.

if (options.size != 4) continue

if (options.none { it.id == answer }) continue

Engine depends on LocalSettingsStore:
```kotlin
interface LocalSettingsStore {
  val quizEnabled: Flow<Boolean>
  suspend fun snapshot(): LocalSnapshot
  suspend fun saveEnabled(enabled: Boolean)
  suspend fun saveElapsedSeconds(seconds: Long)

}

`LocalSettings` also exposes `suspend fun lastQuestionId`: via snapshot.

Engine:
```kotlin
class LocalGuardEngine(
  private val settings: LocalSettingsStore,
  private val playback: PlaybackSource,
  private val scope: CoroutineScope,
  private val intervalSeconds: Long = 900,
  private val tickMillis: Long = 1_000,
  private val persistenceSeconds: Long = 10,
) {
  private val _state = MutableStateFlow(LocalGuardState.IDLE)
  ...
  private val _enabled = MutableStateFlow(true)
  private val _elapsed = MutableStateFlow(0L)
  private val _remaining = MutableStateFlow(intervalSeconds)
  private val _lastQuestionId = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
  private var ready = false

  init {
    scope.launch {
      val snap = settings.snapshot()
      _enabled.value = snap.enabled
      _elapsed.value = snap.elapsedSeconds.coerceIn(0, intervalSeconds)
      _lastQuestionId.value = snap.lastQuestionId
      ready = true
      _state.value = when {
        !snap.enabled -> IDLE
        snap.elapsedSeconds >= intervalSeconds -> QUIZ_REQUIRED
        else -> IDLE
      }
    }
    scope.launch {
      settings.quizEnabled.collect { value ->
        if (!ready) return@collect
        if (_enabled.value == value) return@collect
        _enabled.value = value
        if (!value) {
          _state.value = IDLE
        } else if (_state.value == IDLE) {
          // stays idle until media
        }
        settings.saveEnabled(value) // avoid loop? The flow is from store; saving same value harmless but triggers? DataStore emits same? distinctUntilChanged in implementation; but test fake may loop.
      }
    }
    scope.launch { tickLoop() }
    scope.launch { persistLoop() }
  }
```
Avoid feedback loop: `setEnabled` writes settings; the collector is the source of truth, no need to save inside collector. `setEnabled`:
```kotlin
fun setEnabled(value: Boolean) {
  _enabled.value = value
  if (!value) { _state.value = IDLE; _elapsed.value = 0? } 
  scope.launch { settings.saveEnabled(value); if (!value) settings.saveElapsedSeconds(_elapsed.value) }
}
```
Should turning off reset elapsed? Design says re-enable restarts 15 min. So set elapsed=0 when turning off (or when re-enabling). Do: if off, _elapsed=0 and persist. If on, elapsed remains 0.
- The `quizEnabled` flow collector after restore is unnecessary if setEnabled updates state; but external changes (none) and initial snapshot. We can skip collecting to avoid complexity; settings is only changed via engine. But tests may update fake flow; not necessary. Keep a collector? Simpler skip; engine is sole writer.
- However `setEnabled` called from UI; state updates immediately.

Tick:
```kotlin
private suspend fun tickLoop() {
  while (scope.isActive) {
    delay(tickMillis)
    if (!ready) continue
    if (!_enabled.value) { _state.value = IDLE; continue }
    when (_state.value) {
      QUIZ_REQUIRED, QUIZZING -> continue
      else -> {
        if (playback.mediaActive.value) {
          if (_state.value != LocalGuardState.WATCHING) _state.value = WATCHING
          _elapsed.value = (_elapsed.value + 1).coerceAtMost(intervalSeconds)
          if (_elapsed.value >= intervalSeconds) {
            _state.value = QUIZ_REQUIRED
          }
        } else {
          if (_state.value == WATCHING || _state.value == GRANTED) _state.value = IDLE
        }
      }
    }
    _remaining.value = (intervalSeconds - _elapsed.value).coerceAtLeast(0)
  }
}
```
But if media is not active, elapsed remains and remaining shows. Good.
- When state GRANTED after pass, elapsed=0; next tick sets WATCHING if media active.
- `onQuizStarted`: state QUIZZING.
- `onQuizPassed(questionId)`: lastQuestionId set, elapsed=0, state GRANTED; persist.
- `onQuizCancelled`: if enabled state QUIZ_REQUIRED (so watchdog relaunches).
- `onQuizWrong`: no state change.
- `markQuestionShown(id)`: persist last id? Set on pass or on show? To avoid repeating same question if user quits before answering, set on show. Do in QuizActivity via `engine.rememberQuestion(id)`.
- On restore with elapsed>=interval, state QUIZ_REQUIRED; service will launch. Good.
- `remainingSeconds` state update each tick; initial interval - elapsed.
- `elapsedSeconds` exposed for UI.
- Persistence loop every 10s: save elapsed if changed and enabled; also on pass/off.
- `enabled` state.

Need `playback: PlaybackSource` with `val mediaActive: StateFlow<Boolean>`.

Thread safety: StateFlow.

    private fun updateNotification(text: String) {
        val manager = getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
        manager.notify(NOTIFICATION_ID, buildNotification(text))
    }

    private fun label(state: GuardState): String = when (state) {
        GuardState.UNBOUND -> "设备未激活"
        GuardState.IDLE -> "空闲"
        GuardState.WATCHING -> "观看中"
        GuardState.QUIZ_REQUIRED -> "需要答题：按确认键开始（90 秒未按将自动答题）"
        GuardState.QUIZZING -> "答题中"
        GuardState.GRANTED -> "已授权"
        GuardState.BLOCKED -> "已锁定"
    }

= null): LocalQuestion? {\n        if (questions.isEmpty()) return null\n        if (questions.size == 1 || excludeId.isNullOrBlank()) {\n            return questions[random.nextInt(questions.size)]\n        }\n        val pool = questions.filterNot { it.id == excludeId }\n        if (pool.isEmpty()) return questions[random.nextInt(questions.size)]\n        return pool[random.nextInt(pool.size)]\n    }\n\n    companion object {\n        const val ASSET_NAME = \"questions.json\"\n\n        fun load(context: Context): QuestionBank {\n            val text = runCatching {\n                context.assets.open(ASSET_NAME).bufferedReader(Charsets.UTF_8).use { it.readText() }\n            }.getOrElse { error ->\n                GuardLog.e(\"questions\", \"read $ASSET_NAME failed\", error)\n                return QuestionBank(emptyList())\n            }\n            return fromJson(text)\n        }\n\n        fun fromJson(text: String): QuestionBank {\n            val questions = runCatching { parse(text) }.getOrElse { error ->\n                GuardLog.e(\"questions\", \"parse questions failed\", error)\n                emptyList()\n            }\n            GuardLog.i(\"questions\", \"loaded ${questions.size} local questions\")\n            return QuestionBank(questions)\n        }\n\n        private fun parse(text: String): List<LocalQuestion> {\n            val root = JSONObject(text)\n            val array = root.optJSONArray(\"questions\") ?: JSONArray()\n            val result = ArrayList<LocalQuestion>(array.length())\n            for (index in 0 until array.length()) {\n                val item = array.optJSONObject(index) ?: continue\n                val question = parseQuestion(item) ?: continue\n                result += question\n            }\n            return result\n        }\n\n        private fun parseQuestion(item: JSONObject): LocalQuestion? {\n            val id = item.optString(\"id\").trim()\n            val stem = item.optString(\"stem\").trim()\n            val answer = item.optString(\"answer\").trim()\n            if (id.isEmpty() || stem.isEmpty() || answer.isEmpty()) return null\n\n            val optionsJson = item.optJSONArray(\"options\") ?: return null\n            val options = ArrayList<LocalOption>(optionsJson.length())\n            for (index in 0 until optionsJson.length()) {\n                val option = optionsJson.optJSONObject(index) ?: continue\n                val optionId = option.optString(\"id\").trim()\n                val optionText = option.optString(\"text\").trim()\n                if (optionId.isEmpty() || optionText.isEmpty()) continue\n                options += LocalOption(optionId, optionText)\n            }\n            if (options.size != 4) return null\n            if (options.none { it.id == answer }) return null\n            if (options.map { it.text }.toSet().size != options.size) return null\n\n            return LocalQuestion(\n                id = id,\n                subject = item.optString(\"subject\", \"general\"),\n                stage = item.optString(\"stage\", \"low\"),\n                difficulty

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

Now LocalGuardState + LocalGuardEngine. Make testable.

Design:
```kotlin
enum class LocalGuardState { IDLE, WATCHING, QUIZ_REQUIRED, QUIZZING, GRANTED }
```
Engine:
```kotlin
class LocalGuardEngine(
  private val settings: LocalSettingsStore,
  private val playback: PlaybackSource,
  private val scope: CoroutineScope,
  val intervalSeconds: Long = DEFAULT_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
  private val tickMillis: Long = 1_000L,
  private val persistEveryTicks: Int = 10,
) {
  private val _state = MutableStateFlow(IDLE)
  ...
  private var ready = false
  private var tickCount = 0
  private var lastSavedElapsed = -1L

  init {
    scope.launch { restore() }
    scope.launch { ticker() }
  }
```
Potential race: ticker starts before ready; skip if !ready.

`restore()`:
```kotlin
val snapshot = runCatching { settings.snapshot() }.getOrElse { ... default }
_enabled.value = snapshot.enabled
_elapsed.value = snapshot.elapsedSeconds.coerceIn(0, intervalSeconds)
_lastQuestionId.value = snapshot.lastQuestionId
ready = true
if (!snapshot.enabled) _state.value = IDLE
else if (_elapsed.value >= intervalSeconds) _state.value = QUIZ_REQUIRED
else _state.value = IDLE
_remaining.value = ...
```
If restored elapsed >= interval → QUIZ_REQUIRED, service will launch when collecting (it starts after app; collect gets current). Good.

Ticker:
```kotlin
while (isActive) {
  delay(tickMillis)
  if (!ready) continue
  if (!_enabled.value) { if (_state.value != IDLE) _state.value = IDLE; continue }
  when (_state.value) {
    QUIZZING, QUIZ_REQUIRED -> Unit
    else -> {
      if (playback.mediaActive.value) {
        if (_state.value == IDLE || _state.value == GRANTED) _state.value = WATCHING
        val next = (_elapsed.value + 1).coerceAtMost(intervalSeconds)
        _elapsed.value = next
        if (next >= intervalSeconds) _state.value = QUIZ_REQUIRED
      } else if (_state.value == WATCHING || _state.value == GRANTED) {
        _state.value = IDLE
      }
    }
  }
  _remaining.value = (intervalSeconds - _elapsed.value).coerceAtLeast(0)
  if (++tickCount % persistEveryTicks == 0) persistElapsed()
}
```
- `persistElapsed` launches settings.saveElapsedSeconds if changed.
- On state QUIZ_REQUIRED, persist elapsed = interval.
- On pass: elapsed=0, state GRANTED, remaining=interval, persist.
- On quiz cancelled: state QUIZ_REQUIRED.
- On `rememberQuestion(id)`: set last id + persist.
- `setEnabled(value)`:
```kotlin
if (_enabled.value == value) return
_enabled.value = value
if (!value) { _elapsed.value = 0; _state.value = IDLE; _remaining.value = intervalSeconds }
else { _state.value = if (playback.mediaActive.value) WATCHING else IDLE }
scope.launch { settings.saveEnabled(value); settings.saveElapsedSeconds(_elapsed.value) }
```
- Should turning off reset elapsed? Design says re-enable restarts 15 min → yes.
- Turning on while media playing starts counting.
- `enabled` StateFlow.
- On `onQuizStarted`: `_state.value = QUIZZING` only if enabled? If disabled mid-way, still show.
- On `onQuizPassed(questionId)`: `rememberQuestion(questionId)` then reset.
- `onQuizWrong` no-op.
- `onQuizCancelled`: if enabled _state = QUIZ_REQUIRED else IDLE.
- Expose `elapsedSeconds`, `remainingSeconds`, `lastQuestionId`.
- `setEnabled` called from UI; no suspend.

Note tests: `scope` is `TestScope.backgroundScope`; `delay` uses virtual time. `ready` set in a coroutine launched; use `runCurrent()`. `playback.mediaActive` set true; advanceTimeBy. Good.

Potential persistence race: `persistElapsed` launched each 10 ticks; fine.

Add `fun snapshot()`? no.

Write.

android.content.Intent\nimport android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityEvent\nimport com.openquizgo.guard.ui.QuizActivity\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow\nimport kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow\n\nobject ForegroundTracker {\n    private val _packageName = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)\n    val packageName: StateFlow<String?> = _packageName.asStateFlow()\n\n    fun update(pkg: CharSequence?) {\n        val value = pkg?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()\n        if (value.isNotEmpty() && value != _packageName.value) {\n            _packageName.value = value\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * 无障碍服务：记录前台应用，并提供「从系统绑定服务拉起 Activity」的可靠通道。\n *\n * Android 10+ 对后台启动 Activity 有严格限制；通过无障碍服务启动答题界面\n * 是电视端到点自动弹题的关键路径。\n */\nclass GuardAccessibilityService : AccessibilityService() {\n\n    override fun onServiceConnected() {\n        super.onServiceConnected()\n        instance = this\n        getServiceInfo()?.let { info ->\n            info.flags = info.flags or AccessibilityServiceInfo.FLAG_INCLUDE_NOT_IMPORTANT_VIEWS\n            setServiceInfo(info)\n        }\n        GuardLog.i(\"accessibility\", \"connected\")\n    }\n\n    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {\n        if (event?.eventType != AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED) return\n        val pkg = event.packageName ?: return\n        ForegroundTracker.update(pkg)\n        GuardLog.i(\"foreground=$pkg cls=${event.className}\")\n    }\n\n    override fun onInterrupt() {\n        GuardLog.w(\"accessibility\", \"interrupted\")\n    }\n\n    private fun launchQuiz() {\n        val intent = Intent(this, QuizActivity::class.java)\n            .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP)\n        runCatching { startActivity(intent) }\n            .onSuccess { GuardLog.i(\"accessibility\", \"quiz activity launched\") }\n            .onFailure { GuardLog.e(\"accessibility\", \"quiz launch failed\", it) }\n    }\n\n    override fun onDestroy() {\n        instance = null\n        super.onDestroy()\n    }\n\n    companion object {\n        @Volatile\n        private var instance: GuardAccessibilityService? = null\n\n        fun launchQuizActivity(): Good.

Now tests. Need LocalGuardEngineTest and QuestionBankTest. JVM test cannot use android Context for QuestionBank.load; use `fromJson` with reading asset file directly via File. Test file path `src/main/assets/questions.json`. In Gradle unit tests, working dir is module dir (`app`), so `File("src/main/assets/questions.json")`. Good.

LocalGuardEngineTest:
- FakeSettings implements LocalSettingsStore.
- FakePlayback implements PlaybackSource.
- Use `runTest` with `backgroundScope`; engine created with intervalSeconds=3, tickMillis=1000, persistEveryTicks=1.
- `advanceUntilIdle()` may run forever because tickLoop infinite! `advanceUntilIdle` with infinite delay loop will advance until no tasks—it will keep advancing forever? In `runTest`, `advanceUntilIdle` processes tasks until none; a loop with delay always schedules new tasks → infinite. Avoid advanceUntilIdle. Use `runCurrent()` then `advanceTimeBy(...)`; then `runCurrent()`.
- Because tickLoop starts and delays; restore coroutine launched; runCurrent executes restore. Then set playback true; advanceTimeBy(3000); runCurrent; state should QUIZ_REQUIRED.
- But tick counts: tickLoop starts at t=0, delay(1000) → at t=1000 tick 1; t=2000 tick2; t=3000 tick3 → 3 seconds. Good.
- After state QUIZ_REQUIRED, onQuizStarted/onQuizPassed; check elapsed 0, state GRANTED.
- Toggle off: setEnabled(false), state IDLE, elapsed 0; advance 5s, no trigger.
- Toggle on persisted: fake settings has enabled false; engine restore; state IDLE.
- Persistence: fake settings stores.

Note engine's `scope.isActive` with backgroundScope from runTest; cancels at end.

Test restore with elapsed=2 and interval=3 → after restore state IDLE; advance 1s → QUIZ_REQUIRED.

Fake settings:
```kotlin
class FakeSettings(var enabled: Boolean = true, var elapsed: Long = 0, var last: String? = null): LocalSettingsStore {
  private val enabledFlow = MutableStateFlow(enabled)
  override val quizEnabled = enabledFlow
  override suspend fun snapshot() = LocalSettingsSnapshot(enabled, elapsed, last)
  override suspend fun saveEnabled(e) { enabled=e; enabledFlow.value=e }
  override suspend fun saveElapsedSeconds(s) { elapsed=s }
  override suspend fun saveLastQuestionId(id) { last=id }
}
```
FakePlayback:
```kotlin
class FakePlayback : PlaybackSource { override val mediaActive = MutableStateFlow(false) }
```

Test imports `kotlinx.coroutines.test.*`.

QuestionBankTest reads file; test `fromJson`, assert size 120, random excludes last id (run 50 times), no invalid.

Also test empty JSON returns 0.

Write tests.

File(\"src/main/assets/questions.json\").readText(Charsets.UTF_8)\n\n    @Test\n    fun `内置题库不少于 100 道且结构合法`() {\n        val bank = QuestionBank.fromJson(assetText())\n        assertTrue(\"题库数量不足：${bank.size}\", bank.size >= 100)\n\n        val ids = mutableSetOf<String>()\n        bank.questions.forEach { question ->\n            assertTrue(\"题目 ID 重复：${question.id}\", ids.add(question.id))\n            assertEquals(\"选项数量错误：${question.id}\", 4, question.options.size)\n            assertEquals(\n                \"选项文本重复：${question.id}\",\n                4,\n                question.options.map { it.text }.toSet().size,\n            )\n            assertTrue(\n                \"正确答案不在选项中：${question.id}\",\n                question.options.any { it.id == bank.randomQuestion(last)\n            assertNotNull(question)\n            assertNotEquals(last, question!!.id)\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    fun `空题库不崩溃`() {\n        val bank = QuestionBank.fromJson(\"\"\"{\"questions\":[]}\"\"\")\n        assertEquals(0, bank.size)\n        assertEquals(null, bank.randomQuestion())\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    fun `无效题目被跳过`() {\n        val ## 🤖 助手 · 23:39:15

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

Potential compile issues in test:
- `override val mediaActive: StateFlow<Boolean> = MutableStateFlow(false).asStateFlow()` then cast to MutableStateFlow — casting a StateFlow backed by MutableStateFlow works? `asStateFlow()` returns a read-only wrapper? In kotlinx, `asStateFlow()` returns a `ReadonlyStateFlow` wrapper, not the MutableStateFlow, so cast fails. Fix: define `private val _mediaActive = MutableStateFlow(false); override val mediaActive = _mediaActive.asStateFlow()`.
- `runTest` backgroundScope; `scope.isActive` in tick loop. fine.
- `advanceTimeBy` from kotlinx.coroutines.test as extension on TestScope; imported.
- `persistEveryTicks=1` causes persist coroutine every tick; fine.
- Engine `restore` sets state based on elapsed. In test "答对后计时清零并回到观看态": after `onQuizPassed`, state GRANTED. advance 1s: tick sees state GRANTED, media active → WATCHING. good.
- In "关闭开关": engine.setEnabled(false) sets elapsed 0 and persist; tick loop sees disabled and sets IDLE. advance 10s: each tick `continue` before updating remaining, but no reset. good.
- `runCurrent()` after `advanceTimeBy` maybe redundant but fine.

Fix FakePlayback.

</details>

=== app/src/debug/java/com/openquizgo/guard/debug/DebugReceiver.kt
package com.openquizgo.guard.debug

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import com.openquizgo.guard.GuardApplication
import com.openquizgo.guard.platform.GuardLog

/** 仅 debug 构建可用：adb 触发“需要答题”。 */
class DebugReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {
    override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent?) {
        val app = context.applicationContext as GuardApplication
        when (intent?.action) {
            ACTION_FORCE_QUIZ -> {
                app.container.guardEngine.forceQuizRequired()
                GuardLog.i("debug force quiz required")
            }
            ACTION_FORCE_BLOCK -> {
                app.container.guardEngine.onQuizFailed()
                GuardLog.i("debug force blocked")
            }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_FORCE_QUIZ = "com.openquizgo.guard.DEBUG_FORCE_QUIZ"
        const val ACTION_FORCE_BLOCK = "com.openquizgo.guard.DEBUG_FORCE_BLOCK"
    }
}

## 🤖 助手 · 23:40:37

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

QuestionBank test file path wrong: Gradle unit test working dir is project root (`tv`?) or module? It failed parsing, likely file not found → empty. Check workdir: test task working dir defaults to module directory (`tv/app`). Our path `src/main/assets/questions.json` should exist relative to `tv/app`; but maybe working dir is `tv`. Let's print in test or check. The first test assert bank.size>=100 failed, meaning parse empty or file not found. No exception because `readText` would throw FileNotFoundException (NoSuchElement? no). Actually "随机出题不会连续重复" NoSuchElementException at `bank.questions.first()` means bank empty. So `fromJson(assetText())` returned empty, but `assetText()` didn't throw → file found, but parse returned empty. Why? JSON parse error? Maybe Gson? We use org.json in unit tests, which on JVM is a stub! `org.json` classes in Android SDK are stubs that throw "not mocked" at runtime in unit tests unless `testOptions.unitTests.isReturnDefaultValues=true`? That setting returns default values instead of throwing, so JSONObject methods return null/0 → empty bank. Indeed.

To unit test JSON parsing on JVM, use a real JSON library (org.json:json) as testImplementation, or use kotlinx.serialization. Simpler: add `testImplementation("org.json:json:20240303")` so JVM tests use real implementation. Android runtime uses built-in. Add to build.gradle.

Also the "无效题目" test failed due to same.

Warnings about ExperimentalCoroutinesApi; add `@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)` on test class.

Add dependency and annotation.

Also test file path: if org.json real, check. `File("src/main/assets/questions.json")` under module dir should work. Let's confirm Gradle unit test working dir. It likely is `tv/app` (project dir). If not, we'd see FileNotFound. Since no exception, file found.

Add `testImplementation("org.json:json:20240303")`.

Add opt-in.

</details>

| B. 新增「本地模式」分支，保留服务端代码 | 可随时切回 | 两套状态机并存，复杂且易误触网络 | 否决 |

| D. 单独新建 TV 工程 | 隔离彻底 | 复制权限/无障碍/媒体检测等成熟代码，维护两份 | 否决 |

选型理由：当前阶段产品边界已经收敛为「本地题库 + 定时答题」，方案 C 可以显著降低运行复杂度和故障面；旧服务端代码保留在 Git 历史中，后续需要时再恢复。

## 4. 总体架构

### 4.1 模块划分

| 模块 | 职责 |
| --- | --- |
| `data/local/LocalSettings` | DataStore 保存「定时答题」开关，默认开启 |
| `data/local/QuestionBank` | 读取并解析 `assets/questions.json`，随机取题 |
| `data/foreground/MediaPlaybackDetector` | 通过 `AudioManager` 判断媒体是否在播放 |
| `domain/LocalGuardEngine` | 计时与状态机：关闭/观看/待答题/答题中/通过 |
| `platform/GuardService` | 前台服务、通知、看门狗、到点拉起答题 |
| `platform/GuardAccessibilityService` | 无障碍事件 + 自动拉起答题（后台启动 Activity 的可靠通道） |
| `ui/MainActivity` | 首页：开关、状态、倒计时、权限入口 |
| `ui/QuizActivity` | 单题答题、答错重试、答对退出 |

### 4.2 状态机

```text
           开关关闭
  ┌──────────────────────┐
  ▼                      │
PAUSED ──开关开启──> WATCHING ──播放累计 900s──> QUIZ_REQUIRED
                         ▲                          │
                         │                     拉起答题界面
                         │                          ▼
                         └────答对/重置计时──── QUIZZING
                                                    │
                                              答错：停留本题
```

- `PAUSED`：开关关闭，不计时、不弹题；
- `WATCHING`：开关开启且媒体播放中，按秒累计；
- `QUIZ_REQUIRED`：累计满 900 秒，等待答题界面展示；
- `QUIZZING`：答题界面已展示，计时暂停；
- 答对后回到 `WATCHING` 并把累计秒数清零；答错保持 `QUIZZING`。

### 4.3 触发流程

```text
MediaPlaybackDetector(mediaActive=true)
        │ 每秒
        ▼
LocalGuardEngine 累计 +1s
        │ ≥900s
        ▼
QUIZ_REQUIRED
        │
GuardService 观察到状态 → GuardAccessibilityService.launchQuiz()
        │
        ▼

````

</details>

<summary>思考过程</summary>

Now version bump happens on commit. Before commit, check git status and run final tests/build after all changes. Also remove unused theme Translucent? Not necessary. Check for residual references/warnings. Run `./gradlew :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug :app:assembleRelease` after final edits. Also run `git diff --check`.

Also check app name "电视时间管家" on TV manifest; yes.

Potential issue: `LocalSettings` dataStore delegate uses `context.localGuardDataStore`; AppContainer passes application context? `GuardApplication` passes this (application), yes. MainActivity uses container.

Potential issue: GuardService observes `engine.enabled` and state; `combine` initial emits once after both flows have values (StateFlow immediate). It calls launchQuiz if state QUIZ_REQUIRED on start. Good.
- If accessibility is not enabled at trigger, fallback `startActivity` from service may work on some TVs; toast informs.
- `QuizActivity.isShowing` set true in onCreate before composition; watchdog sees.
- `onQuizStarted` sets QUIZZING, service's state collector sees QUIZZING and does nothing.
- On wrong, state remains QUIZZING, activity stays.
- On correct, state GRANTED; service stops relaunch.
- On activity finish after correct, `onDestroy` doesn't call onQuizCancelled because solved.
- On back, callback consumes. Good.
- On Home, onPause sets isShowing false; watchdog relaunches. Good.
- If user turns off toggle while quiz showing? Cannot access home; no issue.
- After answered, remaining=15 min; elapsed=0.
- On app restart, engine restores elapsed; if elapsed >= 900 and enabled, state QUIZ_REQUIRED; service launched by MainActivity/BootReceiver → collector launches quiz. Good.
- If service restarts while quiz showing (e.g., process death), state QUIZZING restored? We don't persist state; elapsed persisted = 900, restore sets QUIZ_REQUIRED, service relaunches. Fine.
- If service killed during QUIZZING, elapsed persisted 900 → relaunch. Good.
- If toggle off, elapsed persisted 0. Good.

Question bank: `Random.Default` in production. Unit test `randomQuestion(excludeId)` excludes last. Good.
- If the last question is the only one, returns it.

Potential issue: `QuestionBank.fromJson` logs using Android Log in JVM tests; `GuardLog` uses `android.util.Log`, unitTests.isReturnDefaultValues=true, so okay.

Potential issue: `LocalGuardEngine` `persist()` is called from `setEnabled` before `ready` maybe; settings writes fine.

Potential issue: `restore()` and `tickLoop()` race: tick loop ticks after 1s; restore is fast. If restore takes >1s, `ready=false` skip. Fine.

Potential issue: when disabled, tick loop `continue` before updating remaining; remaining stays. Fine.

Potential issue: media detector `isMusicActive` may be true from app's own notification sound? No.

Now run final build/test.

````text
# OpenQuizGo 守护端（Android TV）

正式电视端工程。包名 `com.openquizgo.quiz`（`namespace` 仍为 `com.openquizgo.guard`）。

> 创维等机型会拦截部分包名/新包安装，`com.openquizgo.guard`、`com.openquizgo.tv` 实测会被
> `INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED` 拦截；`com.openquizgo.quiz` 可安装。若更换包名需重新实测。

## 构建

```bash
cd tv
./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
# 产物：app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
```

API 地址默认 `http://192.168.1.27:8080/`，可通过 Gradle 属性覆盖。

### 本地联调（推荐 adb reverse）

部分电视/防火墙会阻止电视反向访问开发机，用 `adb reverse` 把电视的
localhost 映射到开发机，可绕过该问题：

```bash
adb reverse tcp:18080 tcp:18080
./gradlew :app:assembleDebug -PapiBaseUrl=http://127.0.0.1:18080/
```

debug 构建通过 `src/debug/AndroidManifest.xml` 允许明文 HTTP；release 必须使用 HTTPS。

## 测试

```bash
./gradlew :app:testDebugUnitTest
```

## 安装

```bash
adb connect <TV_IP>:5555
adb install -r app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk

# 若遇到 INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED，可重试或：
adb push app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk /data/local/tmp/q.apk
adb shell pm install -r -d /data/local/tmp/q.apk

# 启动
adb shell am start -n com.openquizgo.quiz/com.openquizgo.guard.ui.MainActivity
```

## 当前进度

- P2-T1 工程骨架：Compose for TV、DataStore、Retrofit、手工 DI、CI
- P2-T2 设备激活：激活码、轮询绑定、令牌持久化、重置、离线缓存
- P2-T3 前台识别：无障碍事件 + UsageStats 校正 + 媒体计时 + 诊断页
- P2-T4 守护状态机：前台服务、启动恢复、60s 演示阈值触发需要答题
- P2-T5 答题接管：确认键暂停 + 半透明答题 + 无广告/进度保留
- P2-T6 服务端答题：会话创建、逐题判分、结算授权、断线恢复
- P2-T7 授权心跳：本地倒计时、30s 心跳、暂停不计量、离线补报
- P2-T8 阻挡与看门狗：全屏阻挡、HOME 重拉、家长解锁、90s 超时回退
- P2-T9 权限与诊断：权限向导、自检、能力/篡改上报、最近日志
- 下一步 P2-T10：机型/App 矩阵测试

> 注意：创维机型更新 APK 后会注销无障碍服务，需重启电视或从设置重新开启。

## 目录

```
app/src/main/java/com/openquizgo/guard/
├── GuardApplication.kt
├── di/AppContainer.kt
├── data/local/GuardPreferences.kt
├── data/remote/GuardApi.kt, ApiFactory.kt
└── ui/MainActivity.kt
```

````

````json
{
  "content": "# 电视时间管家（Android TV 本地版）\n\n正式电视端工程。包名 `com.openquizgo.quiz`（`namespace` 仍为 `com.openquizgo.guard`）。\n\n> 创维等机型会拦截部分包名/新包安装，`com.openquizgo.guard`、`com.openquizgo.tv` 实测会被\n> `INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED` 拦截；`com.openquizgo.quiz` 可安装。若更换包名需重新实测。\n\n## 当前模式（077）\n\n- **完全离线**：不连接服务器、不需要激活、不需要家长端；安装并开启无障碍服务后即可使用。\n- 内置 120 道本地单选题（`app/src/main/assets/questions.json`），不按学龄筛选，全部混合随机出题。\n- 首页只有一个开关「定时答题」，默认开启。\n- 累计视频播放 **15 分钟**自动弹出 1 道题：\n  - 答对：显示解析，点「继续观看」退出，计时清零；\n  - 答错：停留本题，返回键无效，直到答对；\n  - 切到桌面/关闭答题界面：守护服务看门狗会自动重新拉起。\n- 关闭开关后不再弹题；重新开启后从 0 开始计时。\n- 开关状态与累计秒数保存在本地 DataStore，重启后恢复。\n\n## 构建\n\n```bash\ncd tv\n./gradlew :app:assembleDebug\n# 产物：app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk\n\n./gradlew :app:assembleRelease\n# 产物：app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release.apk\n```\n\n不再需要 `-PapiBaseUrl`，也没有明文 HTTP 配置。\n\n## 测试\n\n```bash\n./gradlew :app:testDebugUnitTest\n```\n\n覆盖：15 分钟计时、暂停不计时、答对清零、开关关闭、恢复累计时间、题库结构（120 题、4 选项、答案合法）。\n\n## 安装与调试\n\n```bash\nadb connect <TV_IP>:5555\nadb install -r app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk\n\n# 若遇到 INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED，可重试或：\nadb push app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk /data/local/tmp/q.apk\nadb shell pm install -r -d /data/local/tmp/q.apk\n\n# 启动\nadb shell am start -n com.openquizgo.quiz/com.openquizgo.guard.ui.MainActivity\n```\n\n### 立即触发答题（仅 debug）\n\n```bash\nadb shell am broadcast \\\n  -a com.openquizgo.guard.DEBUG_FORCE_QUIZ \\\n  -n com.openquizgo.quiz/com.openquizgo.guard.debug.DebugReceiver\n```\n\n### 关键权限\n\n- **无障碍服务**：用于到点自动拉起答题界面。未开启时首页会提示，无法自动弹题。\n- 不再需要「使用情况访问」与网络权限。\n\n> 注意：部分机型更新 APK 后会注销无障碍服务，需重启电视或从设置重新开启。\n\n## 目录\n\n```\napp/src/main/\n├── assets/questions.json                  # 本地题库\n├── java/com/openquizgo/guard/\n│   ├── GuardApplication.kt\n│   ├── di/AppContainer.kt\n│   ├── data/local/QuestionBank.kt         # 题库加载与随机取题\n│   ├── data/local/LocalSettings.kt        # 开关与计时持久化\n│   ├── data/foreground/MediaPlaybackDetector.kt\n│   ├── domain/LocalGuardEngine.kt         # 本地状态机\n│   ├── platform/GuardService.kt           # 前台服务 + 看门狗\n│   ├── platform/GuardAccessibilityService.kt\n│   └── ui/MainActivity.kt, QuizActivity.kt\n└── res/xml/guard_accessibility.xml\n```\n\n## 题库维护\n\n题库为普通 JSON，字段与约束见\n[`docs/design/077-TV本地题库与定时答题设计.md`](../docs/design/077-TV本地题库与定时答题设计.md)。\n替换 `assets/questions.json` 后重新构建 APK 即可，无需改代码。\n",
  "path": "/Users/mengfeng/我的文档/源码/OpenQuizGo/tv/README.md"
}

## 🤖 助手 · 23:51:46

已按新方向把电视端改成**完全离线的本地单机模式**，并发布 `v1.0.5`。

## 现在的电视端

- 不需要服务器、域名、账号、家长端，安装后即可用。
- 内置 **120 道**本地单选题（数学 40、语文 40、科学常识 25、英语 15）。
- 不按学龄/年级筛选，所有题目混合随机出题，连续两轮不重复。
- 首页只有一个开关：**定时答题**，默认开启。
- 累计视频播放 **15 分钟**自动弹 1 题：
  - 答对：显示解析，点「继续观看」退出，计时清零；
  - 答错：停留本题，返回键无效，直到答对；
  - 按 Home 或切走：守护服务会自动重新拉起答题界面。
- 关闭开关后不再弹题；重新开启从 0 开始计时。
- 开关状态和累计时间保存在本地，重启后恢复。

## 关键文件

| 内容 | 路径 |
| --- | --- |
| 本地题库 | `tv/app/src/main/assets/questions.json` |
| 计时状态机 | `tv/app/src/main/java/com/openquizgo/guard/domain/LocalGuardEngine.kt` |
| 题库加载 | `.../data/local/QuestionBank.kt` |
| 开关持久化 | `.../data/local/LocalSettings.kt` |
| 媒体检测 | `.../data/foreground/MediaPlaybackDetector.kt` |
| 前台服务 | `.../platform/GuardService.kt` |
| 首页 / 答题页 | `.../ui/MainActivity.kt`、`QuizActivity.kt` |

## 已验证

- 15 个 JVM 单测全部通过：计时、暂停不计时、答对清零、开关、恢复、题库结构。
- Debug / Release APK 构建通过。
- Android TV 模拟器实测：
  - 首页开关正常切换；
  - 强制触发答题正常弹出；
  - 答错标红并停留在本题；
  - 答对显示解析并可继续观看；
  - 返回键被拦截；
  - 按 Home 后看门狗自动重新拉起答题。

## 安装包

```text
dist/OpenQuizGo-TV-1.0.5.apk
SHA-256: 7d400e49fbe37631289cd676991208b58e3e88094bdeb366bc6c0ea956dbabd9
```

安装后需要在电视设置里开启**无障碍服务**，否则无法自动弹出答题。

调试时立即触发答题：

```bash
adb shell am broadcast \
  -a com.openquizgo.guard.DEBUG_FORCE_QUIZ \
  -n com.openquizgo.quiz/com.openquizgo.guard.debug.DebugReceiver
```

## 已知限制

- 题库为自建示例题，后续可直接替换 `questions.json` 再打包。
- 媒体播放通过 `AudioManager.isMusicActive` 判断，少数静音播放的播放器可能不计时。
- 尚未在真实电视上做 15 分钟长时播放验证。
- 部分机型更新 APK 后会注销无障碍服务，需要重新开启。

代码已提交、打 tag `v1.0.5`，GitHub 与 Gitee 均已同步。

User asks: "是已经可以在后台静默运行还是必须要打开这个APP保持显示界面？" — Can it run silently in background, or must the app UI stay open?

Need answer based on implementation:
- GuardService is a foreground service started when MainActivity opens and on boot (BootReceiver). It runs in background with a persistent notification. So no need to keep MainActivity in foreground.
- QuizActivity pops up when time due; after answering, it finishes and returns to video app.
- Caveats:
  - First launch needed to start service (unless boot receiver starts on reboot after first install? BootReceiver only on BOOT_COMPLETED; after install, service starts when MainActivity started. If user never opens app after install, service won't start until reboot. So first open once.)
  - Accessibility service must be enabled once.
  - The app may be killed by aggressive TV OEM battery/background management; foreground service + START_STICKY helps but no guarantee. Some TVs kill background apps; may need whitelist/auto-start permission.
  - MainActivity showing "电视时间管家" only when opened; normal operation uses notification, no UI.
  - On some TVs, update APK disables accessibility (noted).
  - GuardService notification: on Android 13+ notifications permission may be needed; TV targetSdk 33; if notification permission not granted, foreground service still runs but notification may be hidden? On Android 13, POST_NOTIFICATIONS is runtime permission; we didn't request it. Without it, FGS notification is not shown in notification drawer but appears in task manager; service still runs. Not a blocker.
  - If the TV is rebooted, BootReceiver starts the service automatically (after first app open/install). RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED granted at install (normal permission).
  - "静默" — it shows a persistent notification (前台服务) and pops quiz UI at 15-min intervals. Not completely silent; quiz is intentionally full-screen. No need to keep app visible.
  - The app's MainActivity can be closed; service continues. But if user uses "force stop" in settings, service stops until next launch/boot.
  - The quiz full-screen will interrupt playback (intended).

Need be accurate about lifecycle:
- `MainActivity.onCreate` calls `GuardService.start(this)` every time.
- `BootReceiver` starts on boot/update.
- `START_STICKY`.
- Watchdog relaunches quiz if dismissed.
- MainActivity is not required to stay open; you can press Home and open a video app.

Potential issue: On Android TV, pressing Home from MainActivity doesn't stop the service; yes.

Also note media detection works while the app is in background because service/container scope is application-level.

可以在后台运行，**不需要一直打开 App 界面**。

## 当前实现

- 打开一次 App 后，`GuardService` 会作为前台服务常驻后台；之后按 Home 键去打开任意视频 App，计时和弹题都正常。
- 到 15 分钟时，答题界面会自动全屏弹出；答对后关闭，回到原来的视频 App。
- 电视重启后，`BootReceiver` 会自动重新启动守护服务。
- 后台常驻会显示一条低优先级通知：**「电视时间管家 · 定时答题」**，这是 Android 前台服务的正常表现，不是 App 界面。

## 但有几个前提

1. **首次安装后要打开一次 App**，并开启无障碍服务；否则服务不会启动，也无法自动弹题。
2. **不要用电视设置里的「强制停止」**，强制停止后服务会停，直到下次打开 App 或重启电视。
3. 部分电视系统会清理后台应用，建议在电视的「应用管理 / 自启动 / 后台运行」里允许「电视时间管家」自启动和后台运行。
4. 更新 APK 后，部分机型会注销无障碍服务，需要重新开启一次。
5. 如果主界面一直开着也没有问题，它只是状态页；关掉不影响后台守护。

简单说：**打开一次、开好无障碍，然后就可以退到后台正常看电视了。**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
APK="${1:-app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release.apk}"
SERIAL="${ANDROID_SERIAL:-}"
ADB=(adb)
[ -n "$SERIAL" ] && ADB+=( -s "$SERIAL" )
"${ADB[@]}" install -r "$APK"
"${ADB[@]}" shell am start -n com.openquizgo.quiz/com.openquizgo.guard.ui.MainActivity
sleep 2
"${ADB[@]}" shell input keyevent 3   # 回到桌面，服务继续后台运行
echo "已安装并启动后台守护；如需自动弹题，请开启无障碍服务。"
```

<summary>🔧 工具调用：write</summary>

```json
{
  "content": "package com.openquizgo.guard.platform\n\nimport android.content.BroadcastReceiver\nimport android.content.Context\nimport android.content.Intent\n\nclass BootReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {\n    override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent?) {\n        when (intent?.action) {\n            Intent.ACTION_BOOT_COMPLETED,\n            ACTION_QUICKBOOT_POWERON,\n            ACTION_HTC_QUICKBOOT_POWERON,\n            Intent.ACTION_MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED -> {\n                GuardLog.i(\"boot\", \"${intent.action} -> start guard service\")\n                runCatching { GuardService.start(context) }\n                    .onFailure { GuardLog.e(\"boot\", \"start guard service failed\", it) }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private companion object {\n        const val ACTION_QUICKBOOT_POWERON = \"android.intent.action.QUICKBOOT_POWERON\"\n        const val ACTION_HTC_QUICKBOOT_POWERON = \"com.htc.intent.action.QUICKBOOT_POWERON\"\n    }\n}\n",
  "path": "/Users/mengfeng/我的文档/源码/OpenQuizGo/tv/app/src/main/java/com/openquizgo/guard/platform/BootReceiver.kt"
}
```

  "path": "/Users/mengfeng/我的文档/源码/OpenQuizGo/docs/design/077-TV本地题库与定时答题设计.md",
  "oldString": "### 4.4 断点与常量",
  "newString": "### 4.4 启动与常驻\n\n- 首次安装后，Android 会把新应用置为 **stopped 状态**，此时不会接收开机广播；\n  必须至少启动一次应用（或通过 `adb` 显式启动组件），这是系统限制，App 无法绕过。\n- 首次启动会拉起 `GuardService` 前台服务，随后按 Home 键即可退到后台；之后不再需要打开主界面。\n- 开机自启由 `BootReceiver` 负责，监听：\n  - `BOOT_COMPLETED`\n  - `QUICKBOOT_POWERON` / `com.htc.intent.action.QUICKBOOT_POWERON`（部分电视的快速开机广播）\n  - `MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED`（APK 升级后）\n- 后台常驻由前台服务 + `START_STICKY` 保证；系统会显示一条低优先级常驻通知，这是 Android 的要求。\n- `MainActivity` 只在用户点击桌面图标时显示设置开关；到点答题只显示全屏 `QuizActivity`，答完即退出。\n- 提供 `tv/scripts/install_and_start.sh`：安装 → 首次启动 → 回到桌面，可选通过 adb 开启无障碍服务。\n- 默认开关为开启；用户关闭后保持关闭，直到手动打开。\n\n### 4.5 断点与常量"
}
```
