## 🤖 助手 · 11:08:39

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

Direct start denied (not exported). So script must launch MainActivity once, then HOME. That's the platform + security boundary. Could add an exported bootstrap activity with no UI to start service and finish, so adb can trigger it without showing settings. That would satisfy "安装后自动后台常驻" for scripted install without UI. Is it worth adding?

Add `BootstrapActivity`:
```kotlin
class BootstrapActivity : Activity() {
  override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    GuardService.start(this)
    finish()
  }
}
```
Manifest:
```xml
<activity android:name=".ui.BootstrapActivity" android:exported="true" android:excludeFromRecents="true" android:theme="@android:style/Theme.NoDisplay" />
```
But `Theme.NoDisplay` requires finish before onResume; we finish in onCreate, okay. Exported activity can be triggered by any app, but it only starts the guard service, low risk. Alternatively protect with a custom permission `com.openquizgo.guard.permission.BOOTSTRAP` (normal permission) — shell won't have it. Keep exported but no sensitive actions; the service itself is not exported.
- Script: `adb shell am start -n .../BootstrapActivity` instead of MainActivity; no UI flash. If bootstrap fails (older APK), fallback to MainActivity.
- This also helps after install: `am start` BootstrapActivity clears stopped state and starts service without showing frontend. Perfect for requirement.
- Does starting an exported activity from shell count as first launch and clear stopped state? Yes.
- On first boot, stopped state cleared; BootReceiver works thereafter.
- The user clicking app icon still opens MainActivity (settings).

Add BootstrapActivity. Test on emulator. This is a clean solution.

Also maybe set `android:noHistory`? `finish()` immediately. Theme.NoDisplay works only if no UI; `finish()` in onCreate before setContent. Use `android:theme="@android:style/Theme.NoDisplay"`. On Android 12, exported activity with NoDisplay is fine.

Implement:
- `ui/BootstrapActivity.kt`.
- Manifest entry.
- Script use BootstrapActivity; fallback to MainActivity if error? `am start` returns success even if activity missing? It prints error. Use:
```bash
if ! adb shell am start -n "$BOOTSTRAP" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  adb shell am start -n "$ACTIVITY" >/dev/null
  sleep 2
  adb shell input keyevent 3
fi
```
But `am start` exits 0 even on error? It usually returns 0 with "Error:" output. Could check output for "Error". Simpler: always use BootstrapActivity (present in this APK).
- Script no need HOME, but sending HOME harmless. Remove HOME or keep? If bootstrap finishes immediately, current foreground stays whatever was before; after install, likely launcher. No UI. No need HOME.

Add.

{
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# 安装并完成后台初始化：\n#   1. 安装 APK；\n#   2. 通过无界面 BootstrapActivity 启动守护服务（不显示任何前端界面，\n#      同时让 Android 退出新装应用的 stopped 状态）；\n#   3. 可选：通过 adb 直接开启无障碍服务（ENABLE_A11Y=1）。\n#\n# 用法：\n#   ./scripts/install_and_start.sh [apk路径]\n#   ENABLE_A11Y=1 ./scripts/install_and_start.sh\nset -euo pipefail\n\ncd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/..\"\n\nPKG=\"com.openquizgo.quiz\"\nBOOTSTRAP=\"$PKG/com.openquizgo.guard.ui.BootstrapActivity\"\nA11Y=\"$PKG/com.openquizgo.guard.platform.GuardAccessibilityService\"\nAPK=\"${1:-app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release.apk}\"\n\nif [[ ! -f \"$APK\" ]]; then\n  echo \"找不到 APK：$APK\"\n  echo \"请先执行：./gradlew :app:assembleRelease\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"安装 $APK\"\nadb install -r \"$APK\"\n\necho \"后台初始化（不显示前端）\"\nadb shell am start -n \"$BOOTSTRAP\" >/dev/null\nsleep 2\n\nif [[ \"${ENABLE_A11Y:-0}\" == \"1\" ]]; then\n  echo \"开启无障碍服务\"\n  adb shell settings put secure enabled_accessibility_services \"$A11Y\"\n  adb shell settings put secure accessibility_enabled 1\nfi\n\necho\necho \"完成：守护服务已在后台运行，开关默认开启。\"\necho \"以后每次开机由 BootReceiver 自动启动，不需要再打开 App。\"\necho \"如需进入设置开关界面，点击电视桌面上的「电视时间管家」即可。\"\n",
  "path": "/Users/mengfeng/我的文档/源码/OpenQuizGo/tv/scripts/install_and_start.sh"
}
